- **Deliverable Scope:**

# PART 1 — COMPREHENSIVE SECURITY & ERROR-SWALLOWING AUDIT (ALL 333 PROJECT FILES)

## 1.1 Executive Summary

Across all `331` files of `TBDMovies_WinUI3` (`+ 2` root repo files = `333` non-doc project files), the core file-organization engine (`PathPolicy`, `SafeTransferEngine`, `FileOperationCoordinator`, `LibraryInstanceGuard`, `NetworkReadGuard`) and primary TMDB/Trakt OAuth flows exhibit strong defensive engineering (reparse-point rejection, same-volume/staged transfers, Bearer-header isolation for TMDB, single-instance lock file, and bounded response stream reading on core Trakt endpoints).

However, newer subsystems (SubDL subtitle downloading, external media player timeline tracking, bidirectional Trakt custom-list/favorites/collection sync, and legacy credential migration/backup sequencing) introduce **26 distinct security, concurrency, data-integrity, and error-swallowing vulnerabilities**:

| Severity | Count | Finding IDs |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **CRITICAL** | **2** | `SEC-19`, `SEC-20` |
| **HIGH** | **9** | `SEC-03`, `SEC-06`, `SEC-08`, `SEC-15`, `SEC-16`, `SEC-21`, `SEC-22`, `SEC-23`, `SEC-24` |
| **MEDIUM** | **11** | `SEC-01`, `SEC-02`, `SEC-04`, `SEC-07`, `SEC-09`, `SEC-10`, `SEC-12`, `SEC-14`, `SEC-18`, `SEC-25`, `SEC-26` |
| **LOW** | **4** | `SEC-05`, `SEC-11`, `SEC-13`, `SEC-17` |
| **Total** | **26** | **`SEC-01` – `SEC-26`** |

## 1.2 Mandatory 5 Focus Files — Line-by-Line Deep Dives

### Focus File 1: `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/SettingsService.cs`

#### `SEC-01` — Missing Ingress Sanitization on Stored & Migrated TMDB Tokens
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/SettingsService.cs:33, 192, 224, 233-235`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `SanitizeAndValidateTmdbToken(string? raw)` (`lines 84-95`) enforces length `[20..2048]` and printable ASCII `[0x20..0x7E]` (blocking CR/LF HTTP header-injection characters, quotes, and non-ASCII corruption), and is applied on UI save (`line 111`) and environment-variable fallback (`line 65` & `AppServices.cs:1065`).
  - However, **three ingress paths bypass `SanitizeAndValidateTmdbToken` completely**:
    1. **Constructor Credential Manager Read (`line 33`):** `var token = credentialStore?.Read()?.Trim();` stores any non-whitespace value from Windows Credential Manager (`TBDMovies/TMDB` or legacy `DropSort/TMDB`) directly into `_tmdbToken` (`line 36`).
    2. **Legacy SQLite Migration (`lines 224, 233-235`):** `MigrateLegacyCredential()` reads `_settings?.Get("tmdb_read_access_token")?.Trim()`, writes it unvalidated to `_credentialStore.Write(legacy)`, and caches `_tmdbToken = legacy`.
    3. **Credential Removal Backup Path (`line 192`):** `RemoveTmdbCredentialAsync` writes `legacy.Trim()` directly into `_credentialStore.Write(legacy.Trim())`.
  - If a legacy SQLite row or manually edited Credential Manager entry contains embedded `\r`/`\n`, surrounding quotes, or control characters (`< 0x20`), `TmdbClient` (`AuthenticationHeaderValue("Bearer", token)`) throws `FormatException` on every TMDB call, which `TmdbClient` (`lines 212, 304, 392, 503, 585`) catches in a bare `catch` and misreports as `MetadataFailureKind.Api` instead of `Authentication`/`NotConfigured`!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Apply `SanitizeAndValidateTmdbToken(...)` uniformly at `line 33`, `line 190-192`, and `line 224`:
    - In the constructor (`line 33`), if `credentialStore?.Read()` is non-null but fails `SanitizeAndValidateTmdbToken`, treat `_tmdbToken = null` and `_tmdbState = TmdbCredentialState.AuthenticationFailed` (or purge corrupt entry).
    - In `MigrateLegacyCredential` (`line 224`), if `legacy` fails `SanitizeAndValidateTmdbToken(legacy)`, delete `"tmdb_read_access_token"` (`line 230`) without writing invalid data to Credential Manager.

#### `SEC-02` — Plaintext `tmdb_read_access_token` Deleted Without `VACUUM;` + Swallowed Cleanup Errors
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/SettingsService.cs:47-54, 153, 195, 230, 236`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `_settings?.Delete("tmdb_read_access_token")` is called at `lines 153, 195, 230, 236`. In SQLite (`WAL` mode), `DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = 'tmdb_read_access_token'` marks the B-tree cell free without zeroing page bytes (`PRAGMA secure_delete` is not enabled in `SqliteConnections.cs`) and appends frames to `library.db-wal`. The plaintext TMDB JWT remains forensically recoverable on disk inside `library.db` freelist pages and `library.db-wal` until a `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE); VACUUM;` runs.
  - Furthermore, `try { MigrateLegacyCredential(); } catch { ... }` (`lines 46-54`) uses a bare `catch` with zero logging: if `_tmdbToken` was already loaded from Credential Manager (`line 36`) and `_settings?.Delete("tmdb_read_access_token")` at `line 230` throws, `_tmdbState` stays `Configured` while the plaintext secret stays in SQLite silently.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - **Option A:** Enable `PRAGMA secure_delete = ON;` on all connections in `SqliteConnections.cs` and checkpoint WAL on credential delete. *Pros:* Zero full-file rewrite cost. *Cons:* Historical WAL frames written before deletion remain until `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)`.
  - **Option B (Recommended Best Option):** Enable `PRAGMA secure_delete = ON;` in `SqliteConnections.cs` **AND** introduce an `ILibraryMaintenanceRepository.ReclaimSecretPages()` method that acquires `DatabaseWriteGate`, executes `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE); VACUUM;` on a configured connection whenever `_settings.Delete("tmdb_read_access_token")` actually removed a row (`rowsAffected > 0`), and persists a `settings` marker (`"maintenance_vacuum_pending" = "1"`) until `VACUUM;` succeeds so transient lock failures are retried automatically on next startup.

#### `SEC-03` — Pre-Migration `DatabaseSafetyBackup` Clones Unpurged Plaintext & DPAPI Secrets Before Cleanup Runs
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:583, 590, 601-603` + `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/Migrations/DatabaseMigrator.cs:776-781`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - Look at the exact startup execution order in `AppServices.Initialize()` (`lines 583-603`):
    1. `line 583`: `new DatabaseMigrator(connectionString).Migrate();` -> When upgrading an existing database (`currentVersion < LatestVersion`), `DatabaseMigrator` (`line 778`) calls `DatabaseSafetyBackup.CreateVerifiedBackup(...)`, copying the entire raw `library.db` into `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TBD Movies\backups\` (retaining up to **5** historical backup `.db` files!).
    2. `line 590`: `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials(settingsRepository, connectionString);` -> Deletes `tmdb_credential_dpapi` and `trakt_credential_dpapi` and runs `VACUUM;` **only on the live `library.db`**.
    3. `line 601`: `new SettingsService(...)` -> Runs `MigrateLegacyCredential()` to delete plaintext `tmdb_read_access_token` **only on the live `library.db`**.
  - Because Step 1 runs **before** Steps 2 and 3, every schema upgrade from an older version permanently snapshots plaintext `tmdb_read_access_token`, legacy DPAPI blobs (`tmdb_credential_dpapi`, `trakt_credential_dpapi`), and plaintext `subdl_api_key` into up to 5 backup files in `backups/` that are **never purged and never `VACUUM`ed**!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - **Option A:** Run credential migration/purge on `library.db` *before* `DatabaseMigrator.Migrate()`. *Pros:* Backup never receives legacy secrets. *Cons:* Touching a pre-migration database before `DatabaseSafetyBackup` violates the migration safety invariant if `settings` doesn't exist yet or the DB has pending FK/journal issues.
  - **Option B (Recommended Best Option):** Keep `DatabaseSafetyBackup` first for crash safety during `Migrate()`, and immediately after `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` + `SettingsService` migration complete (`AppServices.cs:604`), if **any** legacy secret (`tmdb_read_access_token`, `tmdb_credential_dpapi`, `trakt_credential_dpapi`, `subdl_api_key`) was migrated/purged, run a dedicated post-migration scrub over `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TBD Movies\backups\*.db` (`DELETE FROM settings WHERE key IN (...)` + `VACUUM;` on each backup file, or replace the pre-migration backup with a fresh verified post-purge backup and delete the unpurged pre-migration backup).

#### `SEC-04` — Cancellation Bug in `RemoveTmdbCredentialAsync` Implants Legacy Token into Credential Manager
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/SettingsService.cs:190-198`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - When a user clicks **Clear Token**, `RemoveTmdbCredentialAsync` executes:
    ```csharp
    if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(legacy) && _credentialStore.Read() is null)
    {
        _credentialStore.Write(legacy.Trim()); // Line 192: WRITES secret into CredMan!
        if (!string.Equals(_credentialStore.Read(), legacy.Trim(), StringComparison.Ordinal)) return false;
    }
    _settings?.Delete("tmdb_read_access_token"); // Line 195
    cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested(); // Line 196: THROWS BEFORE CredMan Delete!
    _credentialStore.Delete(); // Line 197: NEVER REACHED!
    ```
  - If `cancellationToken` is cancelled between `line 180` and `line 196` (e.g., user closes Settings or cancels), `RemoveTmdbCredentialAsync` **writes** the legacy plaintext token into Windows Credential Manager (`line 192`), deletes the SQLite row (`line 195`), and throws `OperationCanceledException` (`line 196`) **without deleting from Credential Manager** (`line 197`) and **without setting `tmdb_explicitly_removed = "true"`** (`line 199`). On next launch, the credential the user explicitly asked to remove is active in Credential Manager!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Check `cancellationToken.ThrowIfCancellationRequested()` **before** `line 187`, and once mutation begins (`lines 190-202`), execute the entire delete sequence (`_credentialStore.Delete()`, `_settings?.Delete("tmdb_read_access_token")`, `_settings?.Set("tmdb_explicitly_removed", "true", ...)`) non-cancellably inside `_credentialGate` without first writing `legacy` into `_credentialStore` (instead, delete `_credentialStore.Delete()` first, verify `_credentialStore.Read() is null`, then delete `"tmdb_read_access_token"` and set `"tmdb_explicitly_removed"`).

#### `SEC-05` — Dead `catch` Around `_posterCache?.Clear()` Suppresses Incomplete Cleanup Warning
- **Severity:** LOW
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/SettingsService.cs:248-255` + `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Metadata/Cache/DiskPosterCache.cs:313-346`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `SettingsService.ClearLibraryData()` wraps `posterFiles = _posterCache?.Clear() ?? 0;` in `catch (Exception exception) when (exception is IOException or ArgumentException)` (`line 252`) to populate `warning = "POSTER_CACHE_CLEANUP_FAILED"`.
  - However, `DiskPosterCache.Clear()` (`DiskPosterCache.cs:329-345`) wraps per-file `file.Delete()` in a bare `catch { }` (`line 334`) and wraps the entire directory enumeration in a bare `catch { return 0; }` (`line 342`). Consequently, `DiskPosterCache.Clear()` **never** throws `IOException` or `UnauthorizedAccessException` when poster files are locked by WinUI `BitmapImage` handles or permissions fail—making `SettingsService.cs:252` dead code and causing `ClearLibraryDataResult` to falsely report clean completion (`Warning == null`).
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - In `DiskPosterCache.Clear()`, continue best-effort deletion across all files while counting failures (`int failed = 0;`), let outer directory enumeration `IOException`/`UnauthorizedAccessException` propagate, and if `failed > 0` after the loop completes, throw `new IOException($"Failed to delete {failed} cached poster file(s).")`, while expanding `SettingsService.cs:252` to `when (exception is IOException or UnauthorizedAccessException or ArgumentException)`.

### Focus File 2: `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs` (+ `TraktSyncService.ListActions.cs` & `TraktDataClient*.cs`)

#### `SEC-06` — Corrupt JSON Cache Bypasses `!userListsSuccess` Safety Valve & Cascade-Deletes Local Lists
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:713-716, 1666-1686` (plus `742, 762, 782, 802, 822`)
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `ReconcileCustomListsAsync` (`line 1666`) checks `if (!userListsSuccess) return;` as a safety valve so a failed HTTP fetch of `users/me/lists` cannot trick downstream reconciliation into thinking the user deleted all their lists on Trakt.
  - Immediately after (`line 1672`), it calls `var remoteLists = GetLists(accountId);` and deletes **every** local Trakt-linked custom list whose `TraktListId` is absent from `remoteLists` (`_personalLibraryRepository.DeleteCustomListCascade(local.Id);` at `line 1684`).
  - Look at `GetLists(accountId)` (`lines 699-717`): if `_cacheRepository.Get(targetAccount, TraktCacheResourceKeys.Lists)` returns an entry with `PayloadJson == null` (`line 705`) OR throws `JsonException` (`lines 713-716`, e.g., corrupted SQLite text or schema mismatch inside `ParseLists`), `GetLists` silently catches `JsonException` with **zero logging** and returns `Array.Empty<TraktCustomList>()`! Because `userListsSuccess` was `true`, `remoteTraktIds` becomes an empty set `{}` and `lines 1680-1686` **permanently cascade-delete every Trakt-linked custom list and all their members from the user's local database!**
  - The same silent `catch (JsonException) { return Array.Empty<...>(); }` pattern appears in `GetListItems` (`742`), `GetWatchlist` (`762`), `GetWatchedMovies` (`782`), `GetWatchedShows` (`802`), and `GetHistory` (`822`).
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Pass the parsed, validated `IReadOnlyList<TraktCustomList>` directly from Step 4 (`user_lists` fetch in `SyncAsync`) into `ReconcileCustomListsAsync` rather than re-reading and re-parsing SQLite via `GetLists(accountId)`.
  - Additionally, introduce an internal `TryGetLists(accountId, out var lists)` (or throw a typed `TraktCacheCorruptedException` on `JsonException` instead of returning `[]`) so downstream reconciliation **aborts** whenever cache deserialization fails, and call `_cacheRepository.RecordFailure(targetAccount, key, "CORRUPT_CACHE_JSON", ...)` to self-heal on next sync.

#### `SEC-07` — Dead `catch (HttpRequestException)` & Swallowed `401`/`5xx` Failures on Remote List Delete
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1643-1661` + `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Trakt/TraktDataClient.ListWriter.cs:148-168`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - When replaying locally deleted custom lists (`SyncStatus == "PENDING_DELETE"`), `TraktSyncService.cs:1648-1658` writes:
    ```csharp
    catch (HttpRequestException httpEx) when (httpEx.StatusCode == System.Net.HttpStatusCode.NotFound)
    {
        remoteDeletedSuccessfully = true;
    }
    catch (Exception ex)
    {
        System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"[TraktSyncService] Failed to delete remote list {del.TraktListId}: {ex.Message}");
    }
    ```
  - **Why `line 1648` is 100% dead code:** `TraktDataClient.DeleteCustomListAsync` (`TraktDataClient.ListWriter.cs:140-169`) already handles HTTP `404 NotFound` at `lines 156-157` (`if (response.StatusCode == HttpStatusCode.NotFound) return;`), and wraps all transport and non-`404` HTTP failures in `TraktClientException` (`lines 148-151, 166`) — it **never** throws `HttpRequestException`!
  - Meanwhile, `catch (Exception ex)` at `line 1653` catches all real `TraktClientException` failures (including `401 Unauthorized` without `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync`, `429`, and `5xx`) using `Debug.WriteLine` **only** (compiled out in Release), leaving `SyncAsync` reporting `Success = true` with zero diagnostic record when remote list deletion fails!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Remove the dead `catch (HttpRequestException)` at `TraktSyncService.cs:1648` (or guard `TraktClientException` `NotFound` defensively), wrap `DeleteCustomListAsync` inside `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync` so expired access tokens refresh cleanly instead of failing silently, and record non-404 failures in `failedCount` / durable sync diagnostics.

#### `SEC-08` — Offline Queue Data-Loss & Resurrection Bugs (Custom Lists Compaction + Favorites TOCTOU Race)
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1556-1576, 1743-1778, 1881-1910`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Custom Lists Offline Queue Compaction Parity Bug (`lines 1556-1576`):**
     - `ReconcileCustomListsAsync` groups queued `ADD`/`REMOVE` actions by `(ListId, MediaKind, TmdbId)` and executes:
       ```csharp
       if (hasAdd && hasRemove)
       {
           foreach (var action in group) _personalLibraryRepository.DeletePendingAction(action.Id);
       }
       ```
     - Deleting **all** actions whenever both `ADD` and `REMOVE` appear is only valid when the net state equals the pre-offline state! If the user performs **three** toggles while offline:
       - `[ADD, REMOVE, ADD]` -> User ended with the item **in** the list locally, but `hasAdd && hasRemove` deletes all 3 actions (No-Op), **permanently dropping the item from Trakt!**
       - `[REMOVE, ADD, REMOVE]` -> User ended with the item **removed** locally, but `hasAdd && hasRemove` deletes all 3 actions (No-Op); then Step 6.2 (`lines 1695-1725`) pulls the still-present item back down from Trakt via `AddCloudMediaToCustomList`, **resurrecting the item the user deleted!**
  2. **Favorites Offline Queue TOCTOU Race & Silent Wipe (`lines 1743-1778, 1881-1910`):**
     - `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` reads `pendingFavorites = LoadPendingFavoriteActions()` (`line 1881`, acquiring `_pendingFavoritesLock` only for the read), performs slow async HTTP calls (`AddFavoriteAsync`/`RemoveFavoriteAsync`, `lines 1886-1908`) **outside** `_pendingFavoritesLock`, and then overwrites the entire JSON array via `SavePendingFavoriteActions(surviving)` (`line 1909`). Any favorite toggled by the user in the UI (`EnqueuePendingFavoriteAction`, `line 1769`) while those HTTP requests are in flight is **silently overwritten and lost**!
     - Worse, `LoadPendingFavoriteActions` (`line 1753`) has a bare `catch { return new List<TraktPendingFavoriteAction>(); }`: if `EnqueuePendingFavoriteAction` (`line 1773`) encounters a transient read glitch, it overwrites the entire `settings` key with just the single new item, wiping all previously queued actions.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - **For Custom Lists Queue (`lines 1556-1576`):** Order each `(ListId, MediaKind, TmdbId)` group chronologically by `(CreatedAt, Id)` and keep **only the final action** (`var last = group.OrderBy(a => a.CreatedAt).ThenBy(a => a.Id).Last();`), deleting all earlier superseded actions in that group and replaying `last` idempotently (`ADD` or `REMOVE` on Trakt are both idempotent!).
  - **For Favorites Queue (`lines 1743-1910`):**
    - *Option A:* Track a unique `Guid Id` on each `TraktPendingFavoriteAction`; after async HTTP replay finishes, re-acquire `_pendingFavoritesLock`, re-read the live list from `_settingsRepository`, and remove only the specific `Guid` entries that succeeded (preserving any newer actions appended concurrently by the UI).
    - *Option B (Recommended Best Option):* Migrate `trakt_pending_favorite_actions` out of a single JSON blob in `settings` into a proper SQLite table (or extend `pending_sync_actions` with row-level `id PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT` like `custom_list_pending_actions`), eliminating JSON read-modify-write TOCTOU races completely.

#### `SEC-09` — `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` Drops All TV Show Favorites & Overwrites Remote Movie Unfavorites
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1921-1985`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Dead TV Show Favorites Fetch (`lines 1922, 1931, 1934`):** `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` makes an HTTP call to fetch `remoteShowsJson` (`TraktResourceSpec.FavoriteShows()`, `line 1922`), parses `remoteFavShowTmdb` (`line 1934`), and **never references `remoteFavShowTmdb` or `TraktMediaKind.Show` anywhere in `lines 1936-1985`!** TV show favorites are never imported from Trakt and local TV show favorites (`tv_show_personal_state.is_favorite`) are never reconciled upstream.
  2. **Destructive Union Overwrites Remote Unfavoriting (`lines 1944-1983`):** Because `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` performs a naive two-way union (`remote -> local` at `1944-1961`, then `local -> remote` at `1964-1983`) without comparing against a previously synced baseline (`trakt_resource_cache`), if a user unfavorites a movie on `trakt.tv` (web/mobile), `lines 1969-1982` see it is still `PersonalPreference.Liked` locally and **immediately push `AddFavoriteAsync` back to Trakt**, undoing the user's remote action!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Persist the last-synced `favorites_movies` and `favorites_shows` TMDB ID sets in `trakt_resource_cache` (just like `watched_movies`/`user_lists`):
    - Items in `prevSynced - remoteCurrent` were **unfavorited remotely** -> clear local `Liked` / `is_favorite`.
    - Items in `remoteCurrent - prevSynced` were **favorited remotely** -> set local `Liked` / `is_favorite`.
    - Wire `IShowPersonalLibraryRepository` into `TraktSyncService` so `remoteFavShowTmdb` reconciles TV shows identically to movies.

#### `SEC-10` — Sync Steps 5–8 Lack `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync` and Swallow Failures While Reporting `Success = true`
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:603-636, 1468-1471, 1533-1543, 1626-1629, 1653-1656, 1734-1737, 1924-1928, 1956-1959, 1978-1981, 1991-1994, 2014-2018, 2049-2052`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - Steps 1–4 (`SyncResourceAsync`) rigorously use `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync` (retrying once on HTTP `401` and aborting sync with `TraktSyncFailureKind.Unauthorized` on a second `401`), persist per-resource outcomes to `trakt_resource_cache`, and increment `failedCount`.
  - In stark contrast, **Steps 5–8** (`ReconcileDownstreamWatchedAsync`, `ReconcileCustomListsAsync`, `ReconcileFavoritesAsync`, `ReconcileCollectionAsync`, `lines 605-617`):
    1. Call `_authService.GetAccessTokenAsync(forceRefresh: false)` and `_dataClient.*` **directly without `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync`**—so if the access token expires mid-sync after Step 4, Steps 6–8 fail with HTTP `401` without refreshing!
    2. Catch `Exception ex` at every level (`1468, 1533, 1540, 1626, 1653, 1734, 1924, 1956, 1978, 1991, 2014, 2049`) using **`Debug.WriteLine` only** (compiled out in `Release` builds!).
    3. Never increment `failedCount`, never add entries to `outcomes`, and allow `SyncAsync` (`lines 622-636`) to compute `overallSuccess = true`, clear `LastError`, advance `LastSyncSuccessAt`, and display `"Trakt synchronization completed successfully."` in the UI!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Route all HTTP calls in Steps 6–8 through `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync` (propagating `TraktAuthException` / `TraktInvalidGrantException` to abort sync and trigger reconnect, matching Blocker 9 in Steps 1–4).
  - Have each reconciliation method return a `TraktResourceSyncOutcome` (or `bool` success flag) so failures increment `failedCount`, populate `outcomes`, and persist to durable diagnostics.

#### `SEC-11` — Hardcoded `OfficialClientId` on Trakt Writes + Bare `catch` Fabricating `Success = true`
- **Severity:** LOW
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Trakt/TraktDataClient.FavoritesAndCollection.cs:101`; `TraktDataClient.Watchlist.cs:50, 107-110`; `TraktDataClient.Scrobble.cs:136-139`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. `SendBatchSyncPayloadAsync` (`FavoritesAndCollection.cs:101`) and `SendWatchlistMutationAsync` (`Watchlist.cs:50`) hardcode `request.Headers.Add("trakt-api-key", TraktDefaults.OfficialClientId)` instead of accepting the configured `string clientId` parameter (unlike `GetAccountAsync`, `GetResourceAsync`, `SendHistoryMutationAsync`, `ExecuteListMutationAsync`, and `SendScrobbleAsync`, which all pass `clientId`). Users who configure a custom Trakt OAuth app (`SettingsPage` -> `ConfigureTraktAsync`) send an access token issued to their custom `ClientId` paired with `OfficialClientId` in `trakt-api-key`, causing Trakt API `401/403` mismatches on Favorites, Collection, and Watchlist writes!
  2. `Watchlist.cs:107-110` and `Scrobble.cs:136-139` wrap response-stream reading (`ReadBoundedStreamAsync`) and `JsonDocument.Parse` in a bare `catch { return new TraktSyncWriteResult(true, 1); }` / `catch { return new TraktScrobbleResult(true, null, payload.Progress); }`—swallowing caller `OperationCanceledException`, mid-stream `IOException`, oversize `TraktClientException`, and `JsonException` as `Success = true`!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Add `string clientId` to `AddFavoriteAsync`, `RemoveFavoriteAsync`, `AddCollectionBatchAsync`, `AddWatchlistAsync`, and `RemoveWatchlistAsync` on `ITraktDataClient` / `TraktDataClient` and pass `_config.ClientId` from `TraktSyncService`.
  - Replace the bare `catch` blocks at `Watchlist.cs:107` and `Scrobble.cs:136` with explicit `catch (OperationCanceledException) when (ct.IsCancellationRequested) { throw; }` and return `Success: false` on `IOException` / `JsonException` / `TraktClientException`.

### Focus File 3: `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/Migrations/DatabaseMigrator.cs` (+ `DatabaseSafetyBackup.cs`)

#### `SEC-12` — `Migrate()` Fast-Path Skips `WAL`, `foreign_keys`, `busy_timeout`, FK Integrity Check, & Column Drift Checks
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/Migrations/DatabaseMigrator.cs:743-771, 827-935`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - On `99%+` of normal application startups, `library.db` is already at `LatestVersion` (`23`). Look at `lines 743-770`:
    1. `line 743`: Opens a raw `new SqliteConnection(_connectionString)` (NOT `SqliteConnections.Open`, so `busy_timeout` is `0` / default instead of `10000`ms, and `foreign_keys` is `OFF`).
    2. `lines 760-764`:
       ```csharp
       if (currentVersion == LatestVersion && targetVersion == LatestVersion)
       {
           ValidateSchema(connection, exhaustive: false);
           return;
       }
       ```
    3. Because `Migrate()` returns at `line 763` **before** `lines 766-770`:
       - If `library.db` was restored, copied, or opened by an external SQLite tool that switched `journal_mode` back to `DELETE`/`TRUNCATE`, `PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;` (`line 768`) is **never** re-asserted! (Notice `SqliteConnections.Open` only sets `foreign_keys`, `synchronous`, and `busy_timeout`—it relies on `DatabaseMigrator` having persisted `WAL` in the DB header!)
       - `ValidateSchema(connection, exhaustive: false)` skips **all** `RequireTableColumns` drift checks (`lines 856-932`, including the exact `poster_reference` / `custom_display_title` / `custom_lists` drift guards documented at `lines 915-919`!) and skips `EnsureForeignKeyIntegrity` (`line 933`).
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Configure `busy_timeout = 10000` and verify/enforce `journal_mode = WAL` **before** the fast-path `return` (`if (!string.Equals(ReadTextPragma(connection, "journal_mode"), "wal", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)) ExecutePragma(connection, "journal_mode", "WAL");`).
  - Keep `RequireTableColumns` active on the fast path (`PRAGMA table_info` over ~10 local tables takes `< 1.5 ms` in SQLite memory cache!) while keeping only the O(N-rows) full-table `PRAGMA foreign_key_check;` gated to forward migrations and Check Library diagnostics.

#### `SEC-13` — Missing `v23` Column Constraint Validation & Unvalidated Dynamic PRAGMA Helpers
- **Severity:** LOW
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/Migrations/DatabaseMigrator.cs:716-734, 800-804, 898-912, 956, 969, 1011, 1021`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. Migration `v23` (`lines 716-734`) rebuilt `custom_list_pending_actions` specifically to relax `media_kind` and `tmdb_id` from `NOT NULL` (`v22`, `lines 708-709`) to nullable (`v23`, `lines 721-722`) and allow `'CREATE_LIST'` in `action_type`. However, `ValidateSchema` (`lines 909-911`) only checks column *names* (`RequireTableColumns(..., "list_id", "media_kind", "tmdb_id", "action_type", "created_at")`), which already passed on `v22`! If `v23` schema drift leaves `media_kind` or `tmdb_id` `NOT NULL`, `ValidateSchema` passes while offline `CREATE_LIST` actions fail at runtime with `SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_NOTNULL`.
  2. Helper methods `RequireTableColumns` (`line 956`), `ReadIntPragma` (`line 969`), `HasTableColumn` (`line 1011`), and `ExecutePragma` (`line 1021`) interpolate `table` / `name` / `value` directly into `$"PRAGMA ..."` strings. Although all current call sites pass compile-time literals, SQLite does not support bound parameters in `PRAGMA` statements, so a missing allowlist guard creates a latent SQL-injection footgun if a future caller passes dynamic input.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - In `ValidateSchema` (`when i + 1 >= 23` / `includeV23PendingActions`), inspect the `notnull` column (`reader.GetInt32(3) == 0`) from `PRAGMA table_info(custom_list_pending_actions)` for `media_kind` and `tmdb_id`.
  - Add a private `AssertSafeSqlIdentifier(string identifier)` (`Regex.IsMatch(identifier, "^[A-Za-z0-9_]+$")`) at the top of `RequireTableColumns`, `HasTableColumn`, `ReadIntPragma`, and `ExecutePragma`.

#### `SEC-14` — `DatabaseSafetyBackup.PruneOldBackups` Bare `catch` Leaves Secret-Bearing Old Backups on Disk
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/DatabaseSafetyBackup.cs:241-256`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `PruneOldBackups` enforces the 5-backup retention limit on `backups/library-pre-v*.db`, wrapping both directory enumeration and per-file `File.Delete` in a single bare `catch` (`line 253`) with zero logging. Combined with `SEC-03` (backups created before legacy DPAPI/plaintext secrets are purged), if one older backup file is transiently locked by Windows Defender / indexing during migration completion, the loop aborts immediately (`catch` wraps the `foreach`, not individual `File.Delete` calls!) and leaves all older secret-containing `.db` backups on disk silently.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Move the `try/catch` **inside** the `foreach` loop per backup file so one locked file does not abort pruning the remaining expired backups, and log any failed deletion path/reason to durable startup diagnostics.

### Focus File 4: `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs`

#### `SEC-15` — `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` Runs Ungated `VACUUM;` Without `busy_timeout` and Permanently abandons Failed `VACUUM;`
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:590, 1068-1096`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - Examine `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` (`lines 1068-1096`):
    ```csharp
    bool tmdbPurged = WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.MigrateLegacyDpapi(settingsRepository, testRoot);
    bool traktPurged = WindowsTraktCredentialStore.MigrateLegacyDpapi(settingsRepository, testRoot);
    if (tmdbPurged || traktPurged)
    {
        try
        {
            using var connection = new Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.SqliteConnection(connectionString);
            connection.Open();
            using var command = connection.CreateCommand();
            command.CommandText = "VACUUM;";
            command.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine($"VACUUM failed after DPAPI purge: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }
    ```
  - **Four compounding defects occur here:**
    1. **Ungated Write Outside `DatabaseWriteGate`:** `VACUUM;` rewrites the entire database file and requires an exclusive SQLite lock, yet it is executed **without `DatabaseWriteGate.Enter(connectionString)`**!
    2. **Raw `SqliteConnection` Without `SqliteConnections.Open`:** Uses `new SqliteConnection(connectionString)` directly (`line 1080`) without `PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000;` or `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);` (even `VACUUM;` alone leaves old frames in `library.db-wal` until checkpointed!).
    3. **One-Shot Irrecoverable Retry Bug:** `MigrateLegacyDpapi` (`lines 1073-1074`) **already committed `DELETE FROM settings WHERE key = '*_credential_dpapi'`** before `VACUUM;` runs! If `VACUUM;` throws `SqliteException (SQLITE_BUSY)` (e.g., pooled connection from `settingsRepository.Delete` still holding WAL/shared state or antivirus scan) or disk-space `IOException`, `line 1086` swallows it—and on **every future app launch**, `settings.Get("*_credential_dpapi")` returns `null`, `tmdbPurged || traktPurged` is `false`, and **`VACUUM;` is NEVER attempted again!**
    4. **`Debug.WriteLine` Only in Release Builds:** Both `line 1088` and `line 1094` use `System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(...)`, which is `[Conditional("DEBUG")]` and completely stripped from production `Release` binaries—leaving zero trace in `StartupTelemetry` or `logs/`.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Persist `settingsRepository.Set("security_vacuum_pending", "1", UtcNow)` *before* deleting legacy credential keys (`tmdb_credential_dpapi`, `trakt_credential_dpapi`, `tmdb_read_access_token`).
  - Whenever `tmdbPurged || traktPurged || settingsRepository.Get("security_vacuum_pending") == "1"`, acquire `using var gate = DatabaseWriteGate.Enter(connectionString);`, open via `SqliteConnections.Open(connectionString)`, execute `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE); VACUUM;`, and **only after `VACUUM;` succeeds** delete `"security_vacuum_pending"` (followed by another `PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE);`). Record any failure to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TBD Movies\logs\startup_diagnostics.log`.

#### `SEC-16` — `RecoverInterruptedOperations` `"ui_media_folders"` Blindspot, Untrusted Journal Root Self-Approval, & Silent `catch` Swallows
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:771-824` + `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/OperationHistoryService.cs:155-157`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **`"ui_media_folders"` Blindspot (`lines 784-790`):** `RecoverInterruptedOperations` reads only legacy `"ui_movie_folder"` and `"ui_show_folder"` from `settings` and **completely forgets `"ui_media_folders"`** (the newline-separated list used by `AppSettingsStore.ReadMediaFolders` and `DetectStaleTempFiles` at `line 1032`!).
  2. **Security Boundary Inversion — Journal Self-Approves Arbitrary Directories (`lines 805-810` & `OperationHistoryService.cs:155-157`):** Because `"ui_media_folders"` was omitted, `lines 805-810` "worked around" missing roots by unconditionally adding `Path.GetDirectoryName(op.Source)` and `Path.GetDirectoryName(op.Destination)` directly from the `file_operations` database row into `opRoots` before calling `coordinator.Recover(op.Id, opRoots.ToArray())`! `OperationHistoryService.HistoricalRoots` (`lines 155-157`) does the exact same thing during Undo/Reverse!
     - **Impact:** `FileOperationCoordinator` and `PathPolicy.EnsureWithinApprovedRoots` exist specifically to prevent a corrupted or tampered `library.db` journal from moving, overwriting, or deleting files outside user-configured media folders (e.g., `%APPDATA%\Microsoft\Windows\Start Menu\Programs\Startup` or `%USERPROFILE%\.ssh`). Self-approving `Path.GetDirectoryName(op.Source)` and `Path.GetDirectoryName(op.Destination)` from the database row itself **completely neutralizes `approvedRoots` enforcement** during startup crash recovery and Undo!
  3. **Triple Silent Swallow on Crash Recovery (`lines 791, 814, 820`):**
     - `line 791`: Catches root-loading failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only.
     - `line 814`: Empty bare `catch { }` around `coordinator.Recover(op.Id, ...)`—if an interrupted file move fails recovery or hits `RecoveryRequired` (`UnsafeDestination`), **nothing is logged in Debug or Release** and the user is never warned that a media transfer was left half-finished!
     - `line 820`: Empty outer bare `catch { }` swallows `ListNonterminal()` failures completely.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - **Option A:** Add `"ui_media_folders"` parsing to `RecoverInterruptedOperations` and keep `sourceParent`/`destParent` only if they are subdirectories of a configured folder. *Pros:* Simple. *Cons:* Fails recovery if the user legitimately removed a media folder from Settings right before a crash.
  - **Option B (Recommended Best Option):**
    1. Extract a single canonical `ReadApprovedMediaRoots(ISettingsRepository settings)` helper reading `"ui_media_folders"`, `"ui_movie_folder"`, `"ui_show_folder"`, plus `watched_folders` from SQLite, and remove the unconditional `opRoots.Add(sourceParent)` / `opRoots.Add(destParent)` self-approval in **both** `AppServices.cs:805-810` and `OperationHistoryService.cs:155-157`.
    2. If a non-terminal journal entry references a path outside currently approved roots, transition it to `OperationState.RecoveryRequired` (never auto-execute on startup!) and require explicit user confirmation in `OperationsLogPage` showing the exact source/destination paths.
    3. Log all exceptions in `lines 791, 814, 820` to `StartupTelemetry` / `startup_diagnostics.log` and expose `AppServices.PendingRecoveryCount` so `MainWindow` shows a non-blocking `"Interrupted file operation needs review"` `InfoBar`.

#### `SEC-17` — Silent Trakt Startup Disabling & `subdl_api_key` Composition Wiring
- **Severity:** LOW
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:670, 952, 1002-1019, 1044`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `AppServices.cs:670` wires `_subDlClient = new SubDlClient(() => settingsRepository.Get("subdl_api_key"));` directly against plaintext SQLite `settings` (instead of an `ISubDlCredentialStore` backed by Windows Credential Manager like TMDB and Trakt; see `SEC-22`).
  - `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` (`lines 1002-1019`) wraps the entire Trakt service-graph restoration in a bare `catch` with **zero logging** (not even `Debug.WriteLine`!). While isolating local library startup from Trakt failures is good design, silently setting `_trakt = null` with zero diagnostic record or UI status warning leaves users confused as to why their connected Trakt account vanished for the session.
  - `DetectStaleTempFiles` (`line 1044`) swallows `settingsRepository` read failures via `Debug.WriteLine` only, silently scanning only `DataFolder` and missing stale `.tbdmovies-*.tmp` crash remnants in media folders.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Wire `_subDlClient` to a Credential Manager-backed `WindowsSubDlCredentialStore` (`TBDMovies/SubDL`) with one-time migration + gated `VACUUM;` of `"subdl_api_key"`.
  - In `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` (`line 1002`) and `DetectStaleTempFiles` (`line 1044`), preserve startup fault isolation while recording sanitized diagnostics to `startup_diagnostics.log` and exposing `AppServices.TraktStartupWarning` for `SettingsPage`/`TraktPage`.

#### `SEC-18` — Raw `error.Message` Exposed via `AppServices.InitializationError`
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:678-685`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - `catch (Exception error)` (`line 678`) assigns `InitializationError = error.Message;` (`line 680`), which `Require<T>` (`line 1102`) rethrows into `InvalidOperationException` and UI pages display. Raw `SqliteException`, `IOException`, or `DatabaseBackupException` messages include full local filesystem paths, SQLite error internals, and schema details.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Log full `error.GetType().Name` and redacted details to `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TBD Movies\logs\startup_diagnostics.log`, and assign a localized, user-safe message (`MetadataErrorText.For(error)` or `LocalizationService.Text("LibraryInitializationFailed")`) to `InitializationError`.

### Focus File 5: `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FileOperationStore.cs`

#### `SEC-19` — Shared Unsynchronized `SqliteConnection` Thread-Safety & Memory-Corruption Hazard
- **Severity:** **CRITICAL**
- **Exact File:Line:** `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/Repositories/FileOperationStore.cs:9, 26-63, 65-149, 195-212`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - Every other repository in `TBDMovies.Infrastructure` (`MovieRepository`, `MediaFileRepository`, `PersonalLibraryRepository`, `SettingsRepository`, `TraktCacheRepository`, `PlaybackProgressRepository`, `CatalogUnitOfWork`) opens a **short-lived, per-call `SqliteConnection`** via `SqliteConnections.Open(_connectionString)`.
  - **`FileOperationStore` is the sole exception (`line 9`):** `AppServices.Initialize()` (`AppServices.cs:592, 637`) instantiates **one singleton `FileOperationStore`** holding a single `private readonly SqliteConnection _connection;` open for the entire lifetime of the process!
  - `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.SqliteConnection` (and the underlying native `sqlite3*` handle without serialized per-connection locking across ADO.NET `SqliteCommand`/`SqliteDataReader`/`ActiveTransaction` state) is **explicitly NOT thread-safe**:
    1. Write methods `Create` (`line 71`), `Transition` (`line 109`), and `CommitVerified` (`line 120`) acquire `DatabaseWriteGate.Enter(_connectionString)` and start `_connection.BeginTransaction(deferred: false)` on `_connection`.
    2. Read methods `GetById(string id)` (`line 45`), `ListHistory(int limit, int offset)` (`lines 47-56`), and `ListNonterminal()` (`lines 58-63`) execute `_connection.CreateCommand()` and `ExecuteReader()` on the **exact same `_connection` instance with ZERO lock and ZERO `DatabaseWriteGate`!**
    3. Furthermore, `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.SqliteCommand.ExecuteReader()` requires `command.Transaction = _connection.Transaction` whenever an active transaction exists on `_connection`! If a background thread (`OrganizationService` or `LibraryRepairService` via `Task.Run`) is inside `Create`, `Transition`, or `CommitVerified` (`_connection` has an active `SqliteTransaction`!) at the exact moment the UI thread opens `OperationsLogPage` (`BuildEntries` -> `ListHistory()` -> `OperationsLogPage.xaml.cs:125`) or calls `GetById`, `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite` either:
       - Throws `InvalidOperationException("ExecuteReader requires the command to have a transaction when the connection assigned to the command is in a pending local transaction.")` (which `OperationsLogPage.xaml.cs:125` silently swallows into `items = []`!), OR
       - Races inside `SqliteConnection`/`sqlite3_prepare_v2`/`sqlite3_step` across two threads simultaneously, risking `SQLITE_MISUSE` or native heap corruption!
  - In addition, the `FileOperationStore(SqliteConnection connection, ...)` constructor (`lines 26-35`) sets `foreign_keys = ON` and `synchronous = NORMAL`, **omitting `PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000;`**, and `Create`/`Transition`/`CommitVerified` rely solely on implicit `using var transaction` disposal on exception without an explicit `try { ... } catch { try { transaction.Rollback(); } catch { } throw; }` guard against rollback I/O errors masking the primary exception.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - **Option A:** Wrap `_connection` in a private `object _connectionLock = new()` inside `FileOperationStore` around every read (`GetById`, `ListHistory`, `ListNonterminal`) and write (`Create`, `Transition`, `CommitVerified`, `Dispose`). *Pros:* Minimal diff; keeps test constructor `FileOperationStore(SqliteConnection)` working for single-connection `:memory:` unit tests. *Cons:* Still keeps a long-lived open SQLite handle that pins WAL checkpointing (`AppServices.cs:690-695` comment explicitly notes this long-lived connection prevents SQLite from truncating `-wal` until shutdown!).
  - **Option B (Recommended Best Option):**
    1. When constructed via `FileOperationStore(string connectionString, ...)` (production path!), **do NOT hold a long-lived `_connection`**—open a short-lived connection per operation via `using var connection = SqliteConnections.Open(_connectionString);` (matching every other repository in the codebase!), with `using var gate = DatabaseWriteGate.Enter(_connectionString);` on `Create`, `Transition`, and `CommitVerified`, plus explicit `try { ... } catch { try { transaction.Rollback(); } catch { } throw; }`.
    2. When constructed via `FileOperationStore(SqliteConnection injectedConnection, ...)` (in-memory test path), synchronize all accesses to `injectedConnection` under a private `lock (_injectedConnectionSync)` and configure `PRAGMA busy_timeout = 10000;`.
    3. **Double win:** Eliminates the cross-thread `SqliteConnection` race **and** lets SQLite auto-checkpoint `library.db-wal` cleanly during normal runtime instead of growing `-wal` until process exit!

---

## 1.3 Cross-Codebase Security & Error-Swallowing Findings (`SEC-20` – `SEC-26`)

#### `SEC-20` — Subtitle Archive Arbitrary Extension Write, Path Traversal, ZIP Bomb, Cleartext HTTP, Key Leak on Redirect, & Error Swallowing
- **Severity:** **CRITICAL**
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Subtitles/SubtitleDownloadService.cs:41-56, 82-116, 166-195`
  - `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Subtitles/SubDlClient.cs:33, 73, 105-138, 152-160, 176-184, 207-210, 223-235`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Arbitrary File Extension Extraction (`SubtitleDownloadService.cs:92-113`):**
     - `ExtractSubtitleEntry` looks for `.srt`, `.ass`, `.vtt`, and then falls back to **`?? candidateEntries.FirstOrDefault()` (`line 95`)**—selecting **any** file inside the ZIP archive if no `.srt`/`.ass`/`.vtt` is present—and preserves its attacker-controlled extension via `var ext = Path.GetExtension(entry.Name)` (`line 106`)! A malicious or compromised subtitle archive containing `payload.exe`, `Autorun.inf`, `script.bat`, `launch.lnk`, or `codec.dll` extracts and writes that executable directly alongside the user's video file!
  2. **Path Traversal & Arbitrary File Overwrite (`SubtitleDownloadService.cs:43-56, 166-195` + `SubDlClient.cs:223-235`):**
     - `SubDlClient.NormalizeLanguage` (`line 234`) and `SubtitleDownloadService.NormalizeLanguageCode` (`line 194`) return `lower` (`rawLang.Trim().ToLowerInvariant()`) verbatim whenever the API's `"lang"` JSON field is not `"ar"`/`"en"`.
     - `GenerateSubtitlePath` (`line 180`) interpolates `Path.Combine(dir, $"{videoBaseName}.{langCode}{ext}")` with **zero path-separator (`/` or `\`) or `..` validation**, `SaveSubtitleAsync` calls `Directory.CreateDirectory(targetDir)` (`line 48`), and `File.Move(tempPath, targetPath, overwrite: true)` (`line 55`) **overwrites any existing file** without checking `PathPolicy.EnsureWithinApprovedRoots` or `AssertNoReparseComponents`! A crafted `rawLang` such as `"ar/../../../AppData/Roaming/Microsoft/Windows/Start Menu/Programs/Startup/evil"` combined with an `.exe`/`.cmd` ZIP entry escapes `dir` via `Path.GetFullPath` normalization and achieves arbitrary file write / code execution on login!
  3. **Unbounded ZIP Bomb & HTTP Download OOM (`SubtitleDownloadService.cs:102-104` + `SubDlClient.cs:133-138`):**
     - `DownloadArchiveAsync` calls `HttpCompletionOption.ResponseContentRead` + `ReadAsByteArrayAsync` with no byte limit, and `ExtractSubtitleEntry` calls `entryStream.CopyTo(entryMs)` (`line 104`) with no uncompressed size cap—allowing a tiny 50 KB ZIP bomb to exhaust gigabytes of RAM.
  4. **Cleartext `http://` & Arbitrary SSRF/External Host Download + `api_key` Redirect Leak (`SubDlClient.cs:33, 73, 176-184`):**
     - `SubDlClient` constructs `new HttpClient()` (`line 33`) with default `AllowAutoRedirect = true`; `SearchAsync` places `api_key=...` in the URL query string (`line 73`), which .NET `HttpClient` preserves in the target URI or `Referer` across same-scheme/downgrade redirects if the endpoint redirects.
     - `ParseSubtitlesJson` (`lines 176-180`) accepts **any** absolute `http://` (cleartext MITM!) or `https://` URL on **any host** (including `http://127.0.0.1:...` local services like VLC `8088` / MPC `13579` or arbitrary malicious hosts) instead of enforcing `https://` on `*.subdl.com` (`dl.subdl.com`).
  5. **HTTP `429`/`5xx` & API Error Masking (`SubDlClient.cs:116-123, 152-155, 207-210`):**
     - Any non-success status other than `401/403` (including `429 Too Many Requests`, `400 Bad Request`, `500/502/503`) returns `[]` (`line 122`), and `"status": false` JSON error responses from SubDL (which is how SubDL actually reports invalid API keys or rate limits with HTTP `200`!) also return `[]` (`line 154`)—causing `SubtitleDialog.cs:288` to mislead the user with `"No subtitles found"`!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  1. **Strict Extension & Language Allowlist:** Remove `?? candidateEntries.FirstOrDefault()` (`line 95`)! Strictly allow **only** `.srt`, `.ass`, `.ssa`, and `.vtt` extensions; sanitize `langCode` with `Regex.IsMatch(langCode, "^[a-z]{2,3}(-[a-z0-9]{2,4})?$")` (defaulting to `"sub"` otherwise); verify `Path.GetDirectoryName(Path.GetFullPath(targetPath))` equals `Path.GetFullPath(dir)` case-insensitively and call `PathPolicy.AssertNoReparseComponents(targetPath)`.
  2. **Bounded Streams:** Cap `DownloadArchiveAsync` at `10 MB` (`MaxArchiveBytes`) via streamed `HttpCompletionOption.ResponseHeadersRead` and cap `ExtractSubtitleEntry` decompression at `15 MB` (`MaxSubtitleBytes`) + max `100` ZIP entries.
  3. **HTTPS & Host Allowlist + Redirect Hardening:** Configure `SocketsHttpHandler { AllowAutoRedirect = false }` (or allow HTTPS-only redirects strictly within `*.subdl.com` without query-string secrets); reject `rawUrl` unless `uri.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttps` and `uri.Host` is `"dl.subdl.com"` or `"subdl.com"` (or `*.subdl.com`).
  4. **Honest Error Propagation:** When `!response.IsSuccessStatusCode` (`!= 404`) or `status == false` in JSON, read the SubDL `"error"` / `"message"` field and throw a typed `SubtitleServiceException(SubtitleFailureKind)` instead of returning `[]`.

#### `SEC-21` — Credential Store Destructive Migration Loss, Missing `ZeroMemory` on DPAPI Buffers, & Leftover Legacy Targets
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:18, 45-51, 61-88, 169-210`
  - `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:24, 50-56, 66-97, 194-235`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Destructive Credential Deletion When CredMan `Write` Fails (`WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:61-82`, `WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:66-90`):**
     - In `MigrateLegacyDpapi`, `DecryptDpapi` **and** `store.Write(recovered)` sit inside the same `try/catch` (`lines 61-76` / `66-85`), followed **unconditionally** by `settings.Delete("tmdb_credential_dpapi")` / `settings.Delete("trakt_credential_dpapi")`!
     - While a corrupt/foreign-user DPAPI blob (`CryptographicException` inside `DecryptDpapi`) *should* be purged (`[M2]` fault isolation), a transient Windows Credential Manager failure inside `store.Write(recovered)` (e.g., `CredWriteW` lock/session error or roundtrip check failure) means **decryption succeeded on a valid user credential**, `store.Write` failed (`Debug.WriteLine` only!), and `settings.Delete` **permanently destroys the user's only copy of their TMDB token or Trakt OAuth refresh token!**
  2. **Unzeroed Plaintext `byte[] dec` Buffers (`WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:194-203`, `WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:219-228`):**
     - `TryReadTarget` and `Write` carefully call `CryptographicOperations.ZeroMemory(bytes)` in `finally`, **but `DecryptDpapi` allocates `byte[] raw` and plaintext `byte[] dec = ProtectedData.Unprotect(...)` without zeroing either buffer**, leaving decrypted OAuth refresh/access tokens and TMDB JWTs in managed heap memory until GC!
  3. **Legacy `DropSort/*` Credential Manager Target Left Behind (`WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:45-51`, `WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:50-56`):**
     - `Read()` migrates `_legacyTarget` (`DropSort/TMDB` or `DropSort/Trakt`) to `_target` (`TBDMovies/*`) via `Write(...)` (`line 48` / `53`), swallows `Write` failures via `Debug.WriteLine`, and **never calls `CredDeleteW(_legacyTarget, Generic, 0)`**, leaving duplicate credentials lingering under the legacy target name in Windows Credential Manager.
  4. **Dead Code (`EncryptDpapi` & Unused `_settings` Field):** `EncryptDpapi` (`169-183` / `194-208`) and `private readonly ISettingsRepository? _settings;` (`line 18` / `24`) are completely unused remnants of the deprecated DPAPI storage model.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Separate `DecryptDpapi` failure from `store.Write` failure in `MigrateLegacyDpapi`:
    - If `DecryptDpapi` returns `null` (corrupt/unreadable `[M2]` blob), proceed to `settings.Delete(...)`.
    - If `DecryptDpapi` returns a valid secret, call `store.Write(...)` **and verify `store.Read()`**; if `store.Write` throws, log to durable startup diagnostics and **return `false` WITHOUT calling `settings.Delete(...)`** so the credential survives for retry on next launch!
  - Wrap `byte[] dec` (and `raw`) in `try ... finally { CryptographicOperations.ZeroMemory(dec); }` inside `DecryptDpapi`.
  - After `Write(...)` succeeds and `TryReadTarget(_target)` verifies in `Read()`, call `CredDeleteW(_legacyTarget, Generic, 0);`. Remove dead `EncryptDpapi` and unused `_settings` fields.

#### `SEC-22` — Plaintext `subdl_api_key` in SQLite & `PasswordBox` Secret Exposure in `SettingsPage`
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppSettingsStore.cs:11, 27-31, 153, 177`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/SettingsPage.xaml.cs:27-45, 976, 1248-1261`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Plaintext API Key in SQLite (`AppSettingsStore.cs:11, 27-31`):** Unlike TMDB and Trakt credentials (which were hardened into Windows Credential Manager), `subdl_api_key` is stored directly in plaintext inside SQLite `settings` (`Write(SubDlApiKey, trimmed)`), cloned into `DatabaseSafetyBackup` files, and deleted via `_settings.Delete("subdl_api_key")` without `VACUUM;`.
  2. **Plaintext Secret Loaded Back Into `PasswordBox` & Retained Across Page Deactivation (`SettingsPage.xaml.cs:27-45, 1248-1252`):**
     - `SettingsPage` explicitly enforces a security rule for TMDB (`line 212`: never populate saved secrets back into `TmdbTokenBox.Password`, and clear `TmdbTokenBox.Password = string.Empty;` + `TraktClientIdBox.Password = string.Empty;` in `Deactivate()`).
     - `RefreshSubDlStatus()` (`lines 1248-1252`) **violates both rules**: it executes `SubDlApiKeyBox.Password = key ?? string.Empty;` on **every** `SettingsPage.Activate()` and `ApplyLocalization()`, keeping the plaintext API key in UI control memory (and accessible to UI Automation / screen readers / memory dumps), and `Deactivate()` (`lines 27-45`) **forgets to clear `SubDlApiKeyBox.Password = string.Empty;`**!
     - Similarly, `TraktEditConfigButton_Click` (`line 976`) copies `config.ClientId` back into `TraktClientIdBox.Password = config.ClientId;` instead of using a placeholder prompt for replacement.
  3. **False `'Saved'` Confirmation on Silent DB Failure (`SettingsPage.xaml.cs:1257-1260` + `AppSettingsStore.cs:177`):** `AppSettingsStore.Write` swallows all exceptions in an empty `catch (Exception)`, so `SaveSubDlApiKeyButton_Click` displays green `SubDlKeySaved` even when the SQLite write failed!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Store `subdl_api_key` in a dedicated `WindowsSubDlCredentialStore` (`CredWriteW` target `"TBDMovies/SubDL"`, with QA profile hash isolation matching `WindowsTmdbCredentialStore`), migrate any existing SQLite `"subdl_api_key"` row on startup, and trigger gated `VACUUM;`.
  - Never populate `SubDlApiKeyBox.Password` from stored credentials; show a configured/masked status badge (`••••` + Clear button) matching TMDB, clear `SubDlApiKeyBox.Password = string.Empty;` immediately after save and inside `SettingsPage.Deactivate()`, and leave `TraktClientIdBox.Password` empty with placeholder text during `TraktEditConfigButton_Click`.

#### `SEC-23` — External Media Player Shell-Execution Bypass, Command Argument Injection, Static VLC HTTP Password, & Silent MPC WebServer Mutation
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Playback/PlayerProcessLauncher.cs:25-95, 130, 236, 365-377`
  - `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Playback/Trackers/VlcHttpTimelineTracker.cs:22-26`
  - `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Playback/Trackers/MpcWebTimelineTracker.cs:79-162`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/SettingsPage.xaml.cs:300-310`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **`UseShellExecute = true` Bypasses `MediaFormatPolicy`, ADS, & Reparse Checks (`PlayerProcessLauncher.cs:25-29, 73, 94`):**
     - `MediaFileActions.OpenAsync` (`MediaFileActions.cs:23-62`) carefully verifies `MediaFormatPolicy.IsSupportedVideoPath`, rejects NTFS Alternate Data Streams (`HasAlternateDataStream`), and calls `PathPolicy.AssertNoReparseComponents` before launching a file.
     - The newer `PlaybackTrackingService` -> `PlayerProcessLauncher.LaunchAndTrackAsync` (`lines 25-29, 73, 94`) **bypasses `MediaFileActions` completely**, checking only `if (!File.Exists(mediaPath)) return false;` before invoking `Process.Start(new ProcessStartInfo(mediaPath) { UseShellExecute = true })`! If a database row or relinked path points to a `.lnk`, `.bat`, `.exe`, `.hta`, or symlink/junction, Windows Shell executes it directly!
  2. **Command-Line Argument Injection via `.Replace("{path}", mediaPath)` (`PlayerProcessLauncher.cs:66-69, 365-368`):**
     - `BuildCustomArguments` performs naive string replacement: `customArgs.Replace("{path}", mediaPath, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)`. If the user writes `{path}` without quotes (or `mediaPath` contains spaces or `--` flags like `--lua-intf` / `--script` in player-supported filename characters), `ProcessStartInfo.Arguments` splits `mediaPath` into multiple player CLI flags! Furthermore, `SettingsPage.CustomPlayerPathBox_TextChanged` (`lines 300-310`) persists unvalidated text on every keystroke.
  3. **Hardcoded VLC HTTP Password `"tbd_internal"` on Port `8088` (`PlayerProcessLauncher.cs:43-47, 373-377` + `VlcHttpTimelineTracker.cs:22-26`):**
     - Every VLC launch passes `--extraintf http --http-host 127.0.0.1 --http-port 8088 --http-password tbd_internal`. Because `"tbd_internal"` is a compile-time constant known to any local process (and visible in `Win32_Process.CommandLine`), any unprivileged local process—or browser CSRF/DNS-rebinding probe if Basic Auth is cached/Predictable—can query or control the user's running VLC instance (`/requests/status.json?command=...`).
  4. **Silent, Permanent Modification of External MPC-HC / MPC-BE `.ini` & Registry Settings (`MpcWebTimelineTracker.cs:79-162`):**
     - Without asking user permission or disclosing it in the UI, `ConfigureWebInterface` modifies the user's external MPC-HC/MPC-BE installation `.ini` file (`File.WriteAllText`) or `HKCU\Software\MPC-*\Settings` Registry keys to enable `WebServer=1` (an **unauthenticated** localhost HTTP server on port `13579` that exposes currently playing media paths and player commands `/command.html?wm_command=...` to every local process and potential localhost web probes), **never restores the user's original setting on exit**, and hides failures in a bare `catch` (`line 156`).
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  1. Enforce `MediaFormatPolicy.IsSupportedVideoPath(mediaPath)`, `!HasAlternateDataStream(mediaPath)`, and `PathPolicy.AssertNoReparseComponents(mediaPath)` at the entry of `PlayerProcessLauncher.LaunchAndTrackAsync` (`line 25`).
  2. Switch all `ProcessStartInfo` launches (`VLC`, `MPV`, `MPC`, and `Custom`) from flat `Arguments` strings to `startInfo.ArgumentList.Add(...)` so `mediaPath` is always passed as a single OS-escaped argv element (`CommandLineToArgvW`-safe), and validate `CustomPlayerPath` as a rooted `.exe` file on explicit Save/Browse rather than `TextChanged`.
  3. Generate a fresh cryptographic 128-bit random password per VLC launch (`Convert.ToHexString(RandomNumberGenerator.GetBytes(16))`) and pass it to `new VlcHttpTimelineTracker(port, ephemeralPassword)` (and pass via `--http-password` or stdin/env if supported, binding an ephemeral free loopback port instead of fixed `8088`).
  4. Make external player IPC / MPC Web Interface **explicitly opt-in** via a toggle in Settings (with clear disclosure), snapshot the user's prior `WebServer` `.ini`/Registry value before launch, and restore it when the player exits.

#### `SEC-24` — Unredacted `%TEMP%` Crash Log, Open `https://` Launch Without Host Allowlist, & Poster HTTP/Path Hardening
- **Severity:** **HIGH**
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/App.xaml.cs:331-334`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TraktPage.xaml.cs:728-731`
  - `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Metadata/Cache/DiskPosterCache.cs:49, 179-224`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MatchMediaDialog.cs:499-501`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Unredacted Crash Dump in Predictable `%TEMP%` Path (`App.xaml.cs:331-332`):**
     - `OnUnhandledException` writes `$"{DateTimeOffset.Now:O}\n{e.Exception}\n"` via `File.WriteAllText(Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), "tbd_movies_ui_crash.log"), ...)`.
     - `e.Exception.ToString()` includes full stack traces, local media filenames, and—if an HTTP request exception escapes—full request URLs containing `?api_key=...` (`SubDlClient.cs:73`) without URL query-string redaction in a shared predictable `%TEMP%` filename (vulnerable to symlink/hardlink pre-creation on multi-user systems).
  2. **Missing `IsTraktHost` Check on Verification URL Launch (`TraktPage.xaml.cs:728-731`):**
     - `SettingsPage.xaml.cs:1088-1089` and `1240-1241` carefully enforce **both** `uri.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttps` **and** `IsTraktHost(uri.Host)` before launching `deviceCode.VerificationUrl`.
     - `TraktPage.xaml.cs:728-731` checks **only** `uri.Scheme == Uri.UriSchemeHttps`, omitting `IsTraktHost(uri.Host)`! A compromised proxy/custom endpoint or corrupted `DeviceCode` response could open an arbitrary phishing `https://` site when the user clicks the Trakt verification button on `TraktPage`.
  3. **`DiskPosterCache` Auto-Redirect `HttpClient` + `MatchMediaDialog` Unvalidated `PosterReference` (`DiskPosterCache.cs:49`; `MatchMediaDialog.cs:499-501`):**
     - While `DiskPosterCache` properly enforces `DefaultMaxPosterSizeBytes = 10 MiB` (`L14, 173-205`) and `IsValidImage` (`L366-413`), `DiskPosterCache.cs:49` uses `new HttpClient()` with default `AllowAutoRedirect = true` (unlike `TmdbClient`/`TraktDataClient` `[L5]`).
     - `MatchMediaDialog.cs:499-501` constructs `new Uri($"https://image.tmdb.org/t/p/w92/{cleanRef}")` directly for WinUI `BitmapImage` without running `DiskPosterCache`'s strict single-filename regex/path-character check (`DiskPosterCache.cs:355-370`), allowing `#`/`?`/`..` path manipulation inside `image.tmdb.org` requests if a provider candidate returns a malformed `PosterReference`.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  1. Move `tbd_movies_ui_crash.log` into `%LOCALAPPDATA%\TBD Movies\logs\ui_crash.log` (per-user ACLs, matching `StartupTelemetry.cs:48`), and redact `api_key=[^&\s]+` and `Bearer\s+[A-Za-z0-9\-._~+/]+=*` via regex before writing.
  2. Share `ValidateTraktVerificationUrl` (`https://` + `IsTraktHost(uri.Host)`) between `SettingsPage` and `TraktPage.xaml.cs:728`.
  3. Configure `SocketsHttpHandler { AllowAutoRedirect = false }` in `DiskPosterCache.cs:49`, and validate `cleanRef` via `DiskPosterCache.IsSafePosterReference(cleanRef)` before constructing remote `BitmapImage` URIs in `MatchMediaDialog.cs:500`.

#### `SEC-25` — Raw `ex.Message` Leaked Directly to WinUI Dialogs & InfoBars Across 13 Call Sites
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/SubtitleDialog.cs:305, 531`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/SettingsPage.xaml.cs:948, 1063, 1097, 1154, 1206`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TraktPage.xaml.cs:528, 661`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TVShowDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1480`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/OperationsLogPage.xaml.cs:207`
  - `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/TitleInteractionController.cs:228`
  - (Plus `AppServices.cs:680` covered in `SEC-18`)
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  - Although the codebase already provides `MetadataErrorText.For(Exception)` to map internal exceptions into safe, localized user messages, these 13 UI error handlers display raw `ex.Message` directly inside `InfoBar.Message` or `ShowMessageAsync`. In .NET, `HttpRequestException`, `SqliteException`, `IOException`, and `UriFormatException` frequently embed full request URLs (including `?api_key=...` in `SubtitleDialog.cs:305`!), SQLite table/constraint names, and full OS paths.
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Extend `MetadataErrorText.For(Exception)` to cover `TraktAuthException`, `TraktClientException`, and `SubtitleServiceException` with localized resource keys, and route all 13 UI handlers through `MetadataErrorText.For(ex)` (redacting any URL query parameters from diagnostic logs).

#### `SEC-26` — Unobserved Poster `Task.Run` Exceptions & Broken Trakt Custom-List Toggle (Never Removes Items)
- **Severity:** MEDIUM
- **Exact File:Line:**
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Models/MovieModels.cs:191-206`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Models/ShowModels.cs:181-197`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1074-1091`
  - `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/MediaCardContextMenu.cs:275-289`
- **Technical Problem & Failure Mechanism:**
  1. **Unobserved Background `Task.Run` Exceptions (`MovieModels.cs:191-206`, `ShowModels.cs:181-197`):** Both card models launch fire-and-forget `_ = Task.Run(async () => { var path = await AppServices.Poster.EnsurePosterCachedAsync(...); ... });` **without a `try/catch`**! If `AppServices.Poster` throws `InvalidOperationException` (e.g., during shutdown or service reset before `_posterCache` is available) or cancellation occurs, the faulted `Task` goes unobserved until GC finalization (`TaskScheduler.UnobservedTaskException`).
  2. **Broken Trakt Custom-List Toggle & Ignored Result (`MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1074-1091`, `MediaCardContextMenu.cs:275-289`):**
     - Both menus render a `ToggleMenuFlyoutItem` with `IsChecked = inCustomList`. Yet inside the `Click` handler:
       - They **unconditionally call `await trakt.AddMovieToListAsync(customList.TraktId, tmdbId.Value)` even when `inCustomList == true`** (the user unchecked the box to *remove* the movie from the list!)—there is **no `RemoveMovieFromListAsync` branch at all**!
       - Both callers discard the returned `TraktListWriteResult` (`Status == TraktListWriteStatus.Failed`), and `MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1087` swallows exceptions via `Debug.WriteLine` only, leaving the `ToggleMenuFlyoutItem` visually toggled to the wrong state with zero user feedback!
- **Concrete Engineering Fix Proposal:**
  - Wrap the `Task.Run` bodies in `MovieModels.cs:191` and `ShowModels.cs:181` in `try { ... } catch (Exception) { /* optional artwork */ }` matching `ShowModels.cs:742`.
  - Add `RemoveMovieFromListAsync` to `ITraktUiActions` (`TraktSyncService.ListActions.cs`), branch on `inCustomList` in both `MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1083` and `MediaCardContextMenu.cs:284`, inspect `result.Status`, and report failures via `ReportFailure` / `ShowErrorAsync`.

### Positive Security Controls Verified Across Remaining Files (`packaging/`, `tools/`, `Domain/`, `FileSystem/`)
- **Inno Setup & Manifest (`packaging/TBD-Movies.iss`, `src/TBDMovies.UI/app.manifest`):** Runs least-privilege (`PrivilegesRequired=lowest` per-user install under `{localappdata}\Programs\TBD Movies` and `<requestedExecutionLevel level="asInvoker" uiAccess="false"/>`).
- **TMDB Bearer Header Isolation (`src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Metadata/Tmdb/TmdbClient.cs`):** Sends credentials exclusively in `Authorization: Bearer` headers over `https://api.themoviedb.org/3/` (never in URL query strings) and disables auto-redirect credential forwarding risks on custom handlers where injected.
- **Core File Transfer & Path Safety (`PathPolicy.cs`, `SafeTransferEngine.cs`, `FileOperationCoordinator.cs`):** Enforces reparse-point/symlink traversal checks (`FileAttributes.ReparsePoint`), NTFS Alternate Data Stream blocking on `MediaFileActions`, case-insensitive collision guards, SHA-256 cross-volume copy verification before source deletion, and single-instance exclusive lock file (`LibraryInstanceGuard.cs`).
- **QA Seed Tool (`tools/TBDMovies.QaSeed/Program.cs`, `Seed.cs`):** Contains zero hardcoded production tokens/API keys and operates strictly against an explicitly passed local path.

# PART 2 — COMPLETE `try/catch` INVENTORY & CLASSIFICATION (`src/**` + `tools/**`)

## 2.1 Exact Count Verification & `grep` Reconciliation (`547` -> `544` Real C# `catch` Clauses + `3` Comments)

Running `grep -rn "\bcatch\b"` across `TBDMovies_WinUI3/src/` (`546` matches) and `TBDMovies_WinUI3/tools/` (`1` match) yields **`547` raw line matches** (`tests/**` excluded per specification).

Lexical/syntactic verification of all `.cs` files in `src/` and `tools/` confirms:
- **`544` real executable C# `catch` clauses** (`543` in `src/**` + `1` in `tools/**`) — **100% inspected and listed individually in Section 2.4 (`#1` through `#544`) with zero truncation**.
- **`3` single-line C# comments (`//`)** in `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs` that contain the English word `catch` inside `try-catch` prose comments (`544 + 3 = 547` exact reconciliation):

| Grep Match # | File:Line | Exact Comment Line | Classification |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Comment-1 | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:669` | `// Blocker 10: repository calls are outside try-catch to propagate DB/programmer errors` | Non-code C# comment (`//`) |
| Comment-2 | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:707` | `// Blocker 10: catch only JsonException on corrupted payload, not DB/programmer errors` | Non-code C# comment (`//`) |
| Comment-3 | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:736` | `// Blocker 10: catch only JsonException on corrupted payload` | Non-code C# comment (`//`) |

### Strict 3-Category Classification Criteria Applied
- **`(أ) مشكلة` (`99` blocks):**
  - Empty `catch` block or silent swallow where failure matters; **OR**
  - Swallows a security/credential/data-integrity error or masks HTTP auth/network/SQLite repository errors as normal empty results (`[]`, `"NoCustomLists"`, `"No subtitles found"`, `0 missing files`, `Success = true`); **OR**
  - Uses `System.Diagnostics.Debug.WriteLine(...)` **ONLY** on a sensitive or critical path (credentials/tokens, `VACUUM`, SQLite writes/transactions, startup crash/journal file recovery, or Trakt sync data integrity), because `Debug.WriteLine` is `[Conditional("DEBUG")]` and **compiled out completely in `Release` builds**!
- **`(ب) سليمة — بتسجل أو تعالج الخطأ` (`273` blocks):**
  - Persists error to durable storage/telemetry (`trakt_resource_cache`, `FileOperationStore` journal, offline pending queue), rethrows/translates into a typed domain exception or result (`MetadataServiceException`, `TraktClientException`, `TraktAuthException`, `UnsafePathException`, `DatabaseBackupException`, `ImportItemResult`, `ConnectionTestResult`, `FileActionResult`), rolls back savepoints/transactions & propagates, or surfaces an explicit user-facing error state (`InfoBar`, `ContentDialog`, `LibraryViewState.Error`, `LibraryCheckState.Failed`).
- **`(ج) سليمة — عزل أخطاء متعمد وموثق` (`172` blocks):**
  - Intentional, safe fault isolation: documented `[M2]` corrupt legacy DPAPI decryption fallback (`DecryptDpapi`), event-subscriber isolation (`SyncCompleted?.Invoke`, `PlaybackEnded?.Invoke`), expected cancellation lifecycle exit on superseded UI tasks/closed dialogs, best-effort temp-file cleanup in `finally`/rollback paths where throwing would mask the primary exception, optional poster/subtitle/season-title enrichment fallbacks, or cosmetic UI animation/pointer/window-foreground fallbacks that cannot hide data loss or security failures.
