## 2.3 Highlighted Priority List of All `99` `(أ) مشكلة` `catch` Blocks (Grouped by Risk)

### Group 1 — Credentials, `VACUUM`, Startup/Crash Recovery, OAuth Connect/Disconnect, & Secret Backups (Highest Security/Recovery Risk) (`23` `catch` blocks)

| # | File:Line | Method / Operation | Caught Exception | Problem & Concrete Fix |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `#25` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Services/SettingsService.cs:47` | `SettingsService` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` around `MigrateLegacyCredential()` has zero logging; when `_tmdbToken` is already populated from Credential Manager and `_settings.Delete("tmdb_read_access_token")` fails, the plaintext TMDB token remains in SQLite `settings` silently. **Fix:** Log migration/deletion failure to durable startup diagnostics and retry deletion + gated `VACUUM` on next write. |
| `#54` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktAuthService.cs:485` | `DisconnectAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Swallows remote OAuth token revocation failure (`RevokeTokenAsync`) during `DisconnectAsync` with `Debug.WriteLine` only; in Release builds, the user receives 'Disconnected' with zero diagnostic log or warning that the access token remains active on Trakt servers. **Fix:** Return a `RevocationSucceeded` flag on disconnect result and log failure to durable diagnostics. |
| `#241` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Persistence/DatabaseSafetyBackup.cs:253` | `PruneOldBackups` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` in `PruneOldBackups` silently ignores failures to delete old `backups/library-pre-v*.db` files (which contain unpurged legacy plaintext/DPAPI secrets created before credential migration—see SEC-03/SEC-14) with zero logging, allowing sensitive backup copies to accumulate indefinitely on disk. **Fix:** Log pruning failures to durable startup diagnostics and retry pruning on subsequent startups. |
| `#249` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:49` | `Read` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Catches `Write(nativeToken)` failure during legacy `DropSort/TMDB` -> `TBDMovies/TMDB` Credential Manager migration using `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release) and never deletes `_legacyTarget` after successful migration. **Fix:** Delete `_legacyTarget` after verified `Write`, and log migration failures to durable diagnostics. |
| `#250` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:73` | `MigrateLegacyDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **DESTRUCTIVE CREDENTIAL LOSS:** Catches `Exception` around BOTH `DecryptDpapi` AND `store.Write(recovered)` using `Debug.WriteLine` only, then unconditionally executes `settings.Delete("tmdb_credential_dpapi")` at `line 80`! If DPAPI decryption succeeded (valid token) but Credential Manager `Write` threw transiently, the user's TMDB credential is permanently wiped! **Fix:** Separate `DecryptDpapi` `[M2]` corrupt-blob catch (delete corrupt blob) from `store.Write` catch (abort deletion and keep `tmdb_credential_dpapi` for retry on next launch!), plus zero `byte[] dec`. |
| `#251` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:83` | `MigrateLegacyDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Catches `settings.Delete("tmdb_credential_dpapi")` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), leaving legacy DPAPI credential blob in SQLite without any Release-build diagnostic record. **Fix:** Log failure to durable startup diagnostics. |
| `#253` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:178` | `EncryptDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Dead unused `EncryptDpapi` method catches `Exception` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (and leaves `byte[] raw` unzeroed). **Fix:** Delete unused `EncryptDpapi` method completely. |
| `#256` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:54` | `Read` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Catches `Write(nativePayload)` failure during legacy `DropSort/Trakt` -> `TBDMovies/Trakt` Credential Manager migration using `Debug.WriteLine` only and never deletes `_legacyTarget` after migration. **Fix:** Delete `_legacyTarget` after verified `Write`, and log migration failures to durable diagnostics. |
| `#257` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:82` | `MigrateLegacyDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **DESTRUCTIVE CREDENTIAL LOSS:** Catches `Exception` around BOTH `DecryptDpapi` AND `store.Write(recovered)` using `Debug.WriteLine` only, then unconditionally deletes `trakt_credential_dpapi` at `line 89`—permanently destroying valid Trakt OAuth tokens if Credential Manager `Write` fails transiently! **Fix:** Separate `DecryptDpapi` `[M2]` corrupt-blob catch from `store.Write` failure (preserve `trakt_credential_dpapi` when `store.Write` fails!). |
| `#258` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:92` | `MigrateLegacyDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Catches `settings.Delete("trakt_credential_dpapi")` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (silent in Release), leaving legacy Trakt DPAPI tokens in SQLite without Release diagnostics. **Fix:** Log failure to durable startup diagnostics. |
| `#261` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTraktCredentialStore.cs:203` | `EncryptDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Dead unused `EncryptDpapi` method catches `Exception` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (and leaves `byte[] raw` unzeroed). **Fix:** Remove dead `EncryptDpapi` method. |
| `#308` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/MainWindow.xaml.cs:167` | `CompleteStartupAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `CompleteStartupAsync` catches all `Exception` on the background startup orchestration path using `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), without recording failure in `StartupTelemetry` or notifying the shell! **Fix:** Record startup error in `StartupTelemetry` / `logs/startup_telemetry.log` and surface error state on active page. |
| `#316` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:207` | `TraktConfig (get)` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `TraktConfig` getter catches `JsonException`/`SqliteException` with `Debug.WriteLine` only and silently falls back to `(OfficialClientId, DefaultRedirectUri)`, causing custom-app Trakt users to send requests with the wrong `trakt-api-key` if `trakt_api_config` is corrupted or DB read fails! **Fix:** Log to durable diagnostics and surface configuration error rather than silently switching OAuth `ClientId`. |
| `#325` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:791` | `RecoverInterruptedOperations` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `RecoverInterruptedOperations` catches exceptions while loading approved roots from `settings` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (and misses `ui_media_folders` completely—see SEC-16), continuing crash recovery with incomplete root configuration and zero Release diagnostic log. **Fix:** Read `ui_media_folders` via shared parser and record failure in `StartupTelemetry` / `logs/`. |
| `#326` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:814` | `RecoverInterruptedOperations` | `catch (bare)` | Completely empty bare `catch { }` around `coordinator.Recover(op.Id, ...)` silently swallows failed crash recovery of interrupted file moves/renames with zero logging in Debug or Release and zero UI warning! **Fix:** Log recovery failure per operation ID to durable startup diagnostics and surface a 'Recovery Attention Needed' banner linking to Operations Log. |
| `#327` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:820` | `RecoverInterruptedOperations` | `catch (bare)` | Completely empty outer bare `catch { }` in `RecoverInterruptedOperations` silently swallows `operations.ListNonterminal()` SQLite failures with zero logging, completely skipping crash recovery of interrupted file transfers without trace! **Fix:** Log failure to durable startup diagnostics and surface warning in UI. |
| `#328` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:952` | `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Swallows `settingsRepository.Set("trakt_api_config", ...)` failure during Trakt startup migration using `Debug.WriteLine` only. **Fix:** Log to durable startup diagnostics. |
| `#329` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1002` | `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` in `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` cleans up disposable Trakt objects so local library still opens, **but performs zero logging**—silently disabling a user's configured Trakt account on startup without any diagnostic trace! **Fix:** Keep local-library fault isolation, but log exception to `StartupTelemetry`/diagnostics and expose `TraktStartupWarning` on Settings/Trakt pages. |
| `#330` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1044` | `DetectStaleTempFiles` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `DetectStaleTempFiles` catches failures reading `ui_media_folders`/`ui_movie_folder`/`ui_show_folder` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), silently skipping configured media roots when inspecting crash `.tmp` remnants! **Fix:** Log to durable diagnostics and surface partial-scan warning. |
| `#331` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1086` | `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **CRITICAL SECURITY SWALLOW:** Catches `VACUUM;` failure after deleting legacy DPAPI credentials (`PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials`) using `Debug.WriteLine` ONLY (compiled out in Release) on an ungated raw `SqliteConnection`! Because `DELETE` already committed (`lines 80/89`), subsequent launches see no legacy keys and NEVER retry `VACUUM;`, leaving credential remnants in SQLite freelist pages permanently! **Fix:** Execute `VACUUM;` under `DatabaseWriteGate` with configured `busy_timeout`, persist `vacuum_pending = true` flag until `VACUUM;` succeeds, and log failures durably (see SEC-15). |
| `#332` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1092` | `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Outer `catch (Exception ex)` in `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` logs credential-purge failure via `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), hiding failed legacy credential migration/cleanup with zero telemetry. **Fix:** Record failure in durable startup diagnostics (`logs/`). |
| `#541` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TraktPage.xaml.cs:762` | `ConnectTraktButton_Click` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch { return null; }` inside `ConnectTraktButton_Click`'s `Task.Run` silently swallows all `CompleteAuthAsync` exceptions (network errors, expired device code, Credential Manager failure!), closing the auth dialog without showing any error message to the user! **Fix:** Capture the exception and display a localized error in `TraktErrorBanner`. |
| `#543` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TraktPage.xaml.cs:789` | `ConnectTraktButton_Click` | `catch (Exception ex)` | User-initiated `ConnectTraktButton_Click` outer `catch (Exception ex)` logs `BeginAuthAsync` / dialog failures with `Debug.WriteLine` ONLY (compiled out in Release)—when clicking 'Connect Trakt' fails due to network or invalid ClientId, nothing happens on screen! **Fix:** Display localized/sanitized error on `TraktErrorBanner`. |

### Group 2 — Trakt Sync Data Integrity, Dead Catches, Cache Corruption Cascade, & Offline Queues (`32` `catch` blocks)

| # | File:Line | Method / Operation | Caught Exception | Problem & Concrete Fix |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `#4` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Playback/PlaybackTrackingService.cs:241` | `TryScrobbleAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Swallows `TraktScrobbleAction.Stop` failure using `Debug.WriteLine` only; because `TraktSyncService` has no upstream local-to-Trakt watch-history push, a failed `Stop` scrobble permanently drops the watch event on Trakt with zero Release log or retry queue. **Fix:** Enqueue failed `Stop` watch events into an offline Trakt history queue and log via durable diagnostics. |
| `#76` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:713` | `GetLists` | `catch (JsonException)` | `GetLists` silently catches `JsonException` and returns `[]` with no logging or cache invalidation; when `userListsSuccess == true` in `ReconcileCustomListsAsync` (`line 1672`), an empty `remoteLists` from corrupt cached JSON bypasses the safety valve and triggers `DeleteCustomListCascade` wiping local Trakt-linked lists! **Fix:** Return a typed corrupted-cache error or throw/abort downstream deletion when `JsonException` occurs, and mark `trakt_resource_cache` entry failed. |
| `#77` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:742` | `GetListItems` | `catch (JsonException)` | `GetListItems` silently catches `JsonException` and returns `[]` without logging or marking the cached list payload corrupt, masking SQLite cache corruption as an empty list. **Fix:** Record cache parse failure in diagnostics/`trakt_resource_cache` and surface stale/corrupt indicator. |
| `#78` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:762` | `GetWatchlist` | `catch (JsonException)` | `GetWatchlist` silently catches `JsonException` and returns `[]` without logging or marking cache corruption, masking corrupted JSON in `trakt_resource_cache` as an empty watchlist. **Fix:** Record cache parse error and invalidate corrupt cache row. |
| `#79` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:782` | `GetWatchedMovies` | `catch (JsonException)` | `GetWatchedMovies` silently catches `JsonException` and returns `[]` without logging, causing `ReconcileDownstreamWatchedAsync` and Trakt UI to treat corrupted cache JSON as zero watched movies. **Fix:** Log/mark corrupt cache entry and skip downstream reconciliation on parse error. |
| `#80` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:802` | `GetWatchedShows` | `catch (JsonException)` | `GetWatchedShows` silently catches `JsonException` and returns `[]` without logging, masking corrupted cache JSON as zero watched shows. **Fix:** Log/mark corrupt cache entry and skip downstream reconciliation on parse error. |
| `#81` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:822` | `GetHistory` | `catch (JsonException)` | `GetHistory` silently catches `JsonException` and returns `[]` without logging, masking corrupted history cache JSON as empty watch history. **Fix:** Log/mark corrupt cache entry and surface cache error state. |
| `#88` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1150` | `PushMovieWatchedAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `PushMovieWatchedAsync` catches all `Exception` (including `TraktAuthException`/401) with `Debug.WriteLine` only, has no 401 token-refresh retry and no offline queue, and callers ignore the `false` return value—silently dropping watched-history mutations in Release builds. **Fix:** Use `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync`, enqueue failed mutations to an offline history queue, and update auth/sync error state. |
| `#90` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1193` | `PushEpisodeWatchedAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `PushEpisodeWatchedAsync` catches all `Exception` with `Debug.WriteLine` only, lacks 401 refresh retry and offline queueing, and UI callers ignore the `false` return—silently dropping episode watched mutations. **Fix:** Add auth-retry wrapper, offline history queue, and durable error state. |

| `#93` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1236` | `PushMovieWatchlistAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `PushMovieWatchlistAsync` catches all `Exception` with `Debug.WriteLine` only, has no offline watchlist queue or 401 retry, and UI callers discard the `false` return—silently losing watchlist changes. **Fix:** Add offline watchlist pending-actions queue, 401 retry, and durable error reporting. |

| `#96` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1279` | `PushShowWatchlistAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `PushShowWatchlistAsync` catches all `Exception` with `Debug.WriteLine` only, lacks offline queueing and 401 retry, and UI callers ignore `false`—silently losing show watchlist mutations. **Fix:** Add offline watchlist queue, 401 retry, and durable error state. |
| `#101` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1350` | `RunPeriodicSyncLoopAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Outer `catch (Exception ex)` in `RunPeriodicSyncLoopAsync` logs via `Debug.WriteLine` only when `PeriodicTimer` or loop infrastructure throws, permanently terminating background periodic sync for the rest of the app session silently in Release builds. **Fix:** Record failure in `_syncState` / `AppDiagnosticsLog`. |
| `#103` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1468` | `ReconcileDownstreamWatchedAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `ReconcileDownstreamWatchedAsync` (Step 5) catches all `Exception` (including SQLite write failures) with `Debug.WriteLine` only; `SyncAsync` still computes `overallSuccess = true` and clears sync errors! **Fix:** Increment `failedCount` / record step outcome in `outcomes` and log to durable diagnostics. |
| `#104` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1533` | `ReconcileCustomListsAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | When creating a newly linked Trakt custom list (`ReconcileCustomListsAsync`), failure to push an existing local member (`AddMovieToListAsync`/`AddShowToListAsync`) is swallowed with `Debug.WriteLine` only AFTER the list was already marked `SYNCED` (`LinkTraktCustomList` at `line 1508`), and no `ADD` pending action is enqueued—permanently dropping that member from the remote Trakt list! **Fix:** Enqueue a `CustomListPendingAction` (`ADD`) on failure and record partial sync failure. |

| `#123` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1978` | `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Swallows upstream `AddFavoriteAsync` failure during favorites reconciliation with `Debug.WriteLine` only while `SyncAsync` reports full success. **Fix:** Record partial step failure in `failedCount` / diagnostics. |
| `#125` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:1991` | `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` (Step 7) outer `catch (Exception ex)` swallows all exceptions (including `GetAccessTokenAsync` `TraktAuthException`!) with `Debug.WriteLine` only, letting `SyncAsync` report `Success = true`. **Fix:** Rethrow `TraktAuthException`/`TraktInvalidGrantException` and record step failure in `failedCount`. |
| `#126` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:2014` | `ReconcileCollectionAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Swallows `GetResourceAsync` failure for remote collection (without `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync`) using `Debug.WriteLine` only while `SyncAsync` reports `overallSuccess = true`. **Fix:** Use `FetchWithAuthRetryAsync`, rethrow auth failures, and increment `failedCount`. |
| `#128` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Trakt/TraktSyncService.cs:2049` | `ReconcileCollectionAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `ReconcileCollectionAsync` (Step 8) outer `catch (Exception ex)` swallows all exceptions (including `TraktAuthException` and batch upload errors) with `Debug.WriteLine` only while `SyncAsync` reports `Success = true`. **Fix:** Rethrow auth exceptions and record step failure in `failedCount`. |
| `#290` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Trakt/TraktDataClient.Scrobble.cs:136` | `SendScrobbleAsync` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` after HTTP 2xx in `SendScrobbleAsync` swallows `OperationCanceledException` (caller cancel or timeout), `IOException` (truncated stream), and `JsonException`, returning `new TraktScrobbleResult(true, ...)` as if scrobble succeeded! **Fix:** Rethrow `OperationCanceledException` when `cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested`, and return `TraktScrobbleResult(false, ...)` on stream/JSON failure. |
| `#293` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Trakt/TraktDataClient.Watchlist.cs:107` | `SendWatchlistMutationAsync` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` after HTTP 2xx in `SendWatchlistMutationAsync` swallows `OperationCanceledException`, `TraktClientException` (from `ReadBoundedStreamAsync` oversize/stream error), and `JsonException`, fabricating `new TraktSyncWriteResult(true, 1)` even when `added` was `0` (`not_found`) or response stream failed! **Fix:** Rethrow caller cancellation and return `TraktSyncWriteResult(false, 0, ...)` on stream/JSON error. |
| `#414` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1168` | `RemoveHistoryButton_Click` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `RemoveHistoryButton_Click` catches `PushMovieWatchedAsync(false)` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (no offline history-removal queue); on next sync `ReconcileDownstreamWatchedAsync` will re-import the unremoved remote watch event and resurrect watched status locally! **Fix:** Surface warning or enqueue offline `REMOVE_HISTORY` action. |
| `#519` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TVShowDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1260` | `EpisodeMarkWatched_Click` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `EpisodeMarkWatched_Click` catches `PushEpisodeWatchedAsync(false)` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only; without an offline history-removal queue, next Trakt sync (`ReconcileDownstreamWatchedAsync`) resurrects the episode as watched locally! **Fix:** Add offline episode history queue or warn user. |

### Group 3 — Local SQLite Persistence, Playback Tracking, Poster Cache Wipe, & External Player Config Mutation (`10` `catch` blocks)

| # | File:Line | Method / Operation | Caught Exception | Problem & Concrete Fix |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `#2` | `src/TBDMovies.Application/Playback/PlaybackTrackingService.cs:160` | `HandlePlaybackExitAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Swallows SQLite `AddWatchEvent` / `AddEpisodeWatchEvent` write failure at >=90% playback completion using `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), silently losing user watch history. **Fix:** Persist failure to `AppDiagnosticsLog` and surface non-blocking playback-save warning via `PlaybackEnded`/UI notification. |
| `#167` | `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Playback/PlayerProcessLauncher.cs:130` | `LaunchAndTrackAsync` | `catch (Exception)` | Empty `catch (Exception) { }` on the background playback-tracking `Task.Run` silently swallows unhandled exceptions from `RunTrackingLoopAsync` and `onPlaybackEnded` (SQLite playback progress & watch-event persistence) with zero logging. **Fix:** Log exceptions via injected error sink / durable diagnostics instead of empty swallow. |
| `#168` | `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Playback/PlayerProcessLauncher.cs:236` | `RunTrackingLoopAsync` | `catch (bare)` | Empty bare `catch { }` inside `RunTrackingLoopAsync` silently swallows exceptions thrown by `onProgressTick` (SQLite `PlaybackProgressRepository.SaveProgress` writes every few seconds), hiding database lock/corruption failures throughout playback. **Fix:** Separate tracker poll catch from `onProgressTick` persistence errors and log DB failures. |
| `#172` | `src/TBDMovies.FileSystem/Playback/Trackers/MpcWebTimelineTracker.cs:156` | `ConfigureWebInterface` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` silently hides partial/failed unauthorized mutations to external player `.ini` files (`File.WriteAllText`) or `HKCU\Software\MPC-*\Settings` (`WebServer=1`) with zero logging. **Fix:** Remove silent external `.ini`/Registry mutation unless user explicitly opts in, and surface/log configuration failures (see SEC-23). |
| `#198` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Metadata/Cache/DiskPosterCache.cs:334` | `Clear` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch` in `DiskPosterCache.Clear()` silently skips locked/undeletable poster files without tracking failure count, causing `SettingsService.ClearLibraryData()` (`SettingsService.cs:250-255`) to never receive an `IOException` and falsely report complete poster cleanup (`Warning = null`)! **Fix:** Count failed deletions and throw `IOException` after completing best-effort pass (or return `(DeletedCount, FailedCount)`). |
| `#199` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Metadata/Cache/DiskPosterCache.cs:342` | `Clear` | `catch (bare)` | Outer bare `catch` in `DiskPosterCache.Clear()` returns `0` on directory enumeration/access failure (`IOException`/`UnauthorizedAccessException`), making `SettingsService.ClearLibraryData()`'s `catch` at `line 252` dead code and hiding failed cache wipes! **Fix:** Rethrow `IOException` / `UnauthorizedAccessException` so `SettingsService.ClearLibraryData` sets `POSTER_CACHE_CLEANUP_FAILED`. |
| `#333` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppSettingsStore.cs:153` | `Read` | `catch (Exception)` | `AppSettingsStore.Read` catches all `Exception` and returns `null` with zero logging—masking SQLite failures when reading `ui_media_folders`, `subdl_api_key`, and player settings as 'unconfigured'. **Fix:** Log SQLite read failure and propagate or surface degraded-settings state. |
| `#334` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppSettingsStore.cs:177` | `Write` | `catch (Exception)` | `AppSettingsStore.Write` has an empty `catch (Exception)` that silently drops writes to `ui_media_folders`, `subdl_api_key`, and custom player settings! Callers like `SettingsPage.SaveSubDlApiKeyButton_Click` (`line 1257-1260`) immediately display green `'Saved'` feedback even when the database write threw and nothing was saved! **Fix:** Return `bool` / propagate write failure so UI shows an error InfoBar instead of false `'Saved'` confirmation. |
| `#474` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/OperationsLogPage.xaml.cs:125` | `BuildEntries` | `catch (Exception)` | `BuildEntries` catches all `Exception` from `AppServices.History.ListHistory()` (including thread-safety collisions on `FileOperationStore._connection`—see SEC-19!) and sets `items = []`, making the Operations Log page falsely display `'No file operations recorded'` when reading the journal failed! **Fix:** Display error `InfoBar` via `ShowInfo(MetadataErrorText.For(ex), InfoBarSeverity.Error)` instead of masking as empty history. |
| `#522` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TVShowDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1325` | `HandleEpisodeFileMissing` | `catch (bare)` | Empty bare `catch { }` in `HandleEpisodeFileMissing` swallows SQLite `AppServices.MarkMediaFileMissing(file.Id)` write failure while still setting `row.IsFileMissing = true` in the UI—leaving DB state `'PRESENT'` out of sync with UI! **Fix:** Log/surface DB failure instead of empty swallow. |

### Group 4 — API / SQLite Repository Errors Masked as Normal Empty State & Silent UI Action Failures (`34` `catch` blocks)

| # | File:Line | Method / Operation | Caught Exception | Problem & Concrete Fix |
| :---: | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| `#233` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Metadata/Tmdb/TmdbClient.cs:744` | `GetImdbIdAsync` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch { return null; }` in `GetImdbIdAsync` swallows HTTP 401/429/500, `HttpRequestException`, and even `OperationCanceledException` when resolving IMDb IDs for subtitle search, masking broken TMDB auth/network or user cancellation as 'no IMDb ID'. **Fix:** Rethrow `OperationCanceledException` when `cancellationToken.IsCancellationRequested`, and log/propagate `MetadataServiceException`. |
| `#265` | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Subtitles/SubDlClient.cs:207` | `ParseSubtitlesJson` | `catch (JsonException)` | `ParseSubtitlesJson` catches `JsonException` and returns `[]`, masking malformed/truncated SubDL API responses or proxy HTML error pages as `'No subtitles found'` in `SubtitleDialog`. **Fix:** Throw `InvalidDataException` / `SubtitleClientException` so `SubtitleDialog` shows a service/response error instead of false empty results. |
| `#317` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:227` | `GetMovieTmdbId` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch { }` in `GetMovieTmdbId` swallows SQLite repository exceptions and returns `null`, masking database failures as 'movie has no TMDB ID' and silently skipping Trakt mutations. **Fix:** Log repository exception to diagnostics instead of empty bare `catch`. |
| `#318` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:243` | `GetShowTmdbId` | `catch (bare)` | Bare `catch { }` in `GetShowTmdbId` swallows SQLite repository exceptions and returns `null`, masking database failures as 'show has no TMDB ID' and silently skipping Trakt mutations. **Fix:** Log repository exception to diagnostics instead of empty bare `catch`. |
| `#341` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/MediaCardContextMenu.cs:235` | `AddMovieLists` | `catch (Exception)` | `AddMovieLists` catches `Exception` from `ListCustomLists()` and renders disabled `'NoCustomLists'` menu item, masking a SQLite failure as 'user has zero custom lists'. **Fix:** Render localized `'Failed'` / `'CustomListsUnavailable'` error item instead of `'NoCustomLists'`. |
| `#342` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/MediaCardContextMenu.cs:325` | `AddShowLists` | `catch (Exception)` | `AddShowLists` catches `Exception` from `ListCustomLists()` and renders disabled `'NoCustomLists'`, masking SQLite failure as empty custom lists. **Fix:** Render localized `'Failed'` disabled item instead of `'NoCustomLists'`. |
| `#365` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/CheckLibraryPage.xaml.cs:220` | `EpisodeForFile` | `catch (Exception)` | `EpisodeForFile` catches all `Exception` and returns `null` during library health check, masking SQLite lookup errors as unlinked movie/file items. **Fix:** Let repository errors propagate to `RunCheckButton_Click` (`line 357`) which properly transitions `_state = LibraryCheckState.Failed`. |
| `#366` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/CheckLibraryPage.xaml.cs:233` | `EpisodeTotal` | `catch (Exception)` | `EpisodeTotal` catches all `Exception` and returns `0`, masking SQLite errors as `0 episodes`. **Fix:** Propagate DB errors to `RunCheckButton_Click` error state. |
| `#367` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/CheckLibraryPage.xaml.cs:297` | `MissingFiles` | `catch (Exception)` | `MissingFiles()` catches all `Exception` from `AppServices.MissingMediaFiles()` and returns `[]`—causing the **Check Library** diagnostic page to falsely report **zero missing files (healthy library)** when the SQLite query actually threw! **Fix:** Propagate exception or set `_state = LibraryCheckState.Failed` with error banner. |
| `#372` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/HomePage.xaml.cs:158` | `LoadMovies` | `catch (Exception)` | `LoadMovies` catches all `Exception` and returns `[]` with no error banner, masking SQLite catalog failures on HomePage as an empty library (`0 movies`). **Fix:** Record load error flag and display localized error state on HomePage. |
| `#373` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/HomePage.xaml.cs:171` | `LoadShows` | `catch (Exception)` | `LoadShows` catches all `Exception` and returns `[]` with no error state, masking SQLite TV catalog failures on HomePage as `0 shows`. **Fix:** Record load error flag and display localized error state. |
| `#374` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/HomePage.xaml.cs:183` | `LoadHistory` | `catch (Exception)` | `LoadHistory` catches all `Exception` and returns `[]`, silently hiding watch-history repository failures as 'no recent activity'. **Fix:** Surface degraded section state and log error. |
| `#376` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/HomePage.xaml.cs:219` | `BuildContinueWatching` | `catch (bare)` | `BuildContinueWatching` outer bare `catch` returns `[]` with zero logging or UI indicator, hiding SQLite `ViewingActivity`/`PlaybackProgress` failures as 'nothing in Continue Watching'. **Fix:** Log failure and surface section error indicator. |
| `#380` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/LibraryPage.xaml.cs:338` | `GetShowsForRandomPicker` | `catch (bare)` | `GetShowsForRandomPicker` catches all exceptions, sets `shows = []`, and caches `_shows = []` on failure—masking DB read failure as 'no shows available' for Random Picker. **Fix:** Do not cache `[]` on exception; show error notification via `ShowHeaderNotification`. |
| `#382` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/LibraryPage.xaml.cs:750` | `LoadShows` | `catch (Exception)` | Unlike `LoadMovies` (`line 720`, which sets `_state = LibraryViewState.Error`), `LoadShows` catches `Exception`, sets `_shows = null`, and returns `[]` **without** setting `_state = LibraryViewState.Error`, causing TV Shows tab to display an empty library state on database error! **Fix:** Set `_state = LibraryViewState.Error` matching `LoadMovies`. |
| `#386` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/LibraryPage.xaml.cs:1313` | `AddToListFlyout_Opening` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `AddToListFlyout_Opening` catches `ListCustomLists()` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only, leaving the batch 'Add to List' flyout missing user lists with no error item or notification. **Fix:** Show disabled error item or `ShowHeaderNotification(MetadataErrorText.For(ex), isSuccess: false)`. |
| `#408` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:997` | `WatchlistButton_Click` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `WatchlistButton_Click` catches `PushMovieWatchlistAsync` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (and ignores `bool` return); since Watchlist has no offline queue, remote Trakt watchlist silently diverges from local UI in Release builds. **Fix:** Check return/exception and show non-blocking sync warning or enqueue offline watchlist action. |
| `#410` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1041` | `AddToListFlyout_Opening` | `catch (Exception)` | `AddToListFlyout_Opening` catches `ListCustomLists()` failure and displays `'NoCustomLists'`, masking a SQLite error as 'no custom lists exist'. **Fix:** Display localized `'Failed'` disabled item instead of `'NoCustomLists'`. |
| `#411` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1063` | `AddToListFlyout_Opening` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Trakt Watchlist toggle in `AddToListFlyout_Opening` catches failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (no offline queue, ignores `bool` result), silently dropping the user's explicit menu click when offline or unauthorized. **Fix:** Surface error via `ReportFailure` and/or offline watchlist queue. |
| `#412` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1087` | `AddToListFlyout_Opening` | `catch (Exception ex)` | Trakt Custom List click in `AddToListFlyout_Opening` always calls `AddMovieToListAsync` (even when `inCustomList == true`, never removing—see SEC-26!), ignores the returned `TraktListWriteResult` (`Status == Failed`), and catches exceptions with `Debug.WriteLine` only—completely hiding failed user actions! **Fix:** Branch on `inCustomList` to add/remove, inspect `result.Status`, and surface failures via `ReportFailure`. |
| `#416` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MovieDetailsPage.xaml.cs:1203` | `AddWatch` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `AddWatch` catches `PushMovieWatchedAsync(true)` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (no offline history queue), silently dropping Trakt watch sync. **Fix:** Enqueue offline history action or surface sync warning. |
| `#439` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MyListsPage.Trakt.cs:137` | `RefreshTraktAsync` | `catch (bare)` | `RefreshTraktAsync` bare `catch` replaces a failed `BuildSnapshotAsync` with an empty `TraktLocalSnapshot([])`, causing every local movie/show on the Trakt lists view to falsely display as non-local/cloud-only without any warning banner! **Fix:** Track `snapshotFailed = true` (like `TraktPage.xaml.cs:407`) and show a degraded-local-match warning. |
| `#452` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MyListsPage.xaml.cs:127` | `SyncNowButton_Click` | `catch (Exception ex)` | User-initiated `SyncNowButton_Click` catches `Exception` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (and ignores `TraktSyncResult` abort/failure!), so clicking 'Sync Now' fails silently in Release builds with zero UI feedback! **Fix:** Inspect `TraktSyncResult` and report exceptions via `await ReportAsync(ex)`. |
| `#453` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MyListsPage.xaml.cs:154` | `LoadCustomLists` | `catch (Exception)` | `LoadCustomLists` catches all `Exception` and returns `[]`, masking SQLite failures as 'user has zero custom lists' in the sidebar. **Fix:** Surface error state via `ReportAsync` / sidebar error indicator. |

| `#461` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MyListsPage.xaml.cs:867` | `RemoveFromSelectedList` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `RemoveFromSelectedList` catches `PushMovieWatchlistAsync(false)` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (no offline watchlist queue), silently failing to remove movie from Trakt Watchlist. **Fix:** Enqueue offline watchlist action or notify user. |
| `#469` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MyListsPage.xaml.cs:1141` | `ShowAddMediaAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `ShowAddMediaAsync` catches `PushShowWatchlistAsync(true)` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (no offline watchlist queue). **Fix:** Enqueue offline watchlist action or notify user. |
| `#470` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/MyListsPage.xaml.cs:1160` | `ShowAddMediaAsync` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `ShowAddMediaAsync` catches `PushMovieWatchlistAsync(true)` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only (no offline watchlist queue). **Fix:** Enqueue offline watchlist action or notify user. |
| `#503` | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Views/TVShowDetailsPage.xaml.cs:441` | `ToggleShowPersonal` | `catch (Exception ex)` | `ToggleShowPersonal` catches `PushShowFavoriteAsync` / `PushShowWatchlistAsync` failure with `Debug.WriteLine` only; for Watchlist there is no offline queue, and for Show Favorites `ReconcileFavoritesAsync` (`TraktSyncService.cs:1934`) never reconciles TV shows anyway! **Fix:** Implement TV show favorites reconciliation + offline watchlist queue and surface errors. |

| 250 | `src/TBDMovies.Infrastructure/Security/WindowsTmdbCredentialStore.cs:73` | `MigrateLegacyDpapi` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **(أ) مشكلة** | **DESTRUCTIVE CREDENTIAL LOSS:** Catches `Exception` around BOTH `DecryptDpapi` AND `store.Write(recovered)` using `Debug.WriteLine` only, then unconditionally executes `settings.Delete("tmdb_credential_dpapi")` at `line 80`! | 325 | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:791` | `RecoverInterruptedOperations` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **(أ) مشكلة** | `RecoverInterruptedOperations` catches exceptions while loading approved roots from `settings` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (and misses `ui_media_folders` completely—see SEC-16), continuing crash recovery with incomplete root configuration and zero Release diagnostic log. **Fix:** Read `ui_media_folders` via shared parser and record failure in `StartupTelemetry` / `logs/`. |

| 329 | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1002` | `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` | `catch (bare)` | **(أ) مشكلة** | Bare `catch` in `TryRestoreTraktOnStartup` cleans up disposable Trakt objects so local library still opens, **but performs zero logging**—silently disabling a user's configured Trakt account on startup without any diagnostic trace! **Fix:** Keep local-library fault isolation, but log exception to `StartupTelemetry`/diagnostics and expose `TraktStartupWarning` on Settings/Trakt pages. |
| 330 | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1044` | `DetectStaleTempFiles` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **(أ) مشكلة** | `DetectStaleTempFiles` catches failures reading `ui_media_folders`/`ui_movie_folder`/`ui_show_folder` with `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), silently skipping configured media roots when inspecting crash `.tmp` remnants! **Fix:** Log to durable diagnostics and surface partial-scan warning. |
| 331 | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1086` | `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **(أ) مشكلة** | **CRITICAL SECURITY SWALLOW:** Catches `VACUUM;` failure after deleting legacy DPAPI credentials (`PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials`) using `Debug.WriteLine` ONLY (compiled out in Release) on an ungated raw `SqliteConnection`! Because `DELETE` already committed (`lines 80/89`), subsequent launches see no legacy keys and NEVER retry `VACUUM;`, leaving credential remnants in SQLite freelist pages permanently! **Fix:** Execute `VACUUM;` under `DatabaseWriteGate` with configured `busy_timeout`, persist `vacuum_pending = true` flag until `VACUUM;` succeeds, and log failures durably (see SEC-15). |
| 332 | `src/TBDMovies.UI/Services/AppServices.cs:1092` | `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` | `catch (Exception ex)` | **(أ) مشكلة** | Outer `catch (Exception ex)` in `PurgeLegacyDpapiCredentials` logs credential-purge failure via `Debug.WriteLine` only (compiled out in Release), hiding failed legacy credential migration/cleanup with zero telemetry. **Fix:** Record failure in durable startup diagnostics (`logs/`). |
